Abstract: As large language models are increasingly deployed in sensitive domains, anonymizing input data to protect personally identifiable information has become a critical practice. However, the impact of this anonymization on model utility is not well understood. This paper presents a systematic empirical study of the trade-off between privacy and performance. We evaluate five prominent language models across eleven diverse benchmarks, comparing their performance on original versus pseudonymized inputs. Our results reveal that while anonymization generally degrades performance, the effect is highly nuanced. We find that more capable models, such as Qwen2.5-72B and GPT-4o mini, suffer the largest performance drops, suggesting a stronger reliance on specific entity information. The impact is also task-dependent: performance on TruthfulQA improves with anonymization, while retrieval-focused tasks like RGB experience a catastrophic decline. Further experiments show that reversible anonymization techniques that preserve entity uniqueness significantly outperform irreversible ones like redaction, and that explicitly prompting models about anonymization offers no discernible benefit. We conclude that anonymization is not a one-size-fits-all solution and must be co-designed with the model and task in mind to balance privacy and utility effectively. Our findings provide a crucial baseline for developing more robust, privacy-aware AI systems.

</details>


### [114] [SEAR: Segment-Evidence-Aware Routing for Weak-to-Strong Multilingual Speech MCQ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11355)
*Huy Hoang Le,Long-Bao Nguyen,Minh Tri Dao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文为MLC-SLM Challenge Task 2提出基于Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct的多语言对话语音理解系统，通过分段证据感知的数据与后训练流水线构建35万余条多选问答数据，结合SFT与GSPO强化学习，官方评测集准确率达90.92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言对话语音场景下，模型需要理解带时间戳的语音并回答相关问题。现有数据与训练流程难以保证问题真正依赖音频证据，且文本可答与需音频推理的样本混杂，影响训练效果。作者希望构建证据可靠、难度分层的多语言语音问答数据与后训练方案。

Method: 首先用语言模型把带时间戳的ASR转成连贯事件跨度，按边界余量扩展并从原始录音裁剪出音频证据段。再用Qwen3.6-27B生成语义类MCQ，用Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite生成声学类MCQ，并做结构、证据接地、答案一致性及目标模型可训练性四类校验，得到21种语言/口音变体共359,825条验证MCQ。随后用纯文本探针将数据分为弱样本（文本可答）与强样本（依赖音频），前者做SFT，后者用GSPO强化学习，并引入去偏优势、序列级重要性校正与动态过滤以稳定训练。

Result: 在最终官方评测集上取得90.92%的准确率。

Conclusion: 分段证据感知的数据构建、严格的多重校验以及“文本弱样本SFT+音频强样本GSPO”的混合后训练流程，能有效提升多语言对话语音语言模型的问答性能，验证了该方案的有效性。

Abstract: This paper describes our system for Task~2 of the second Multilingual Conversational Speech Language Model (MLC-SLM) Challenge. We adapt Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct with a segment-evidence-aware data and post-training pipeline. A language model converts timestamped ASR into coherent event spans, which are expanded by a boundary margin and cropped from the original recording. We then synthesize complementary semantic MCQs with Qwen3.6-27B and acoustic MCQs with Gemini~3.1 Flash-Lite, followed by structural, grounding, answer-consistency, and target-model trainability checks, yielding 359,825 verified MCQs across 21 language and accent variants. A text-only probe partitions the data into weak, text-answerable items used for supervised fine-tuning and strong, audio-dependent items used for reinforcement learning with Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO), stabilized by debiased advantages, sequence-level importance correction, and dynamic filtering. Our system obtains 90.92% accuracy on the final official evaluation set.

</details>


### [115] [TransClean: A Benchmark for Detecting and Extracting Clean Translations from Large Language Model Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11399)
*Shenbin Qian,Yves Scherrer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了LLM翻译输出中的"翻译噪声"问题，分析了79万余条翻译输出，归纳出12种噪声模式，并构建了TransClean基准（9,900对噪声-干净样本）及两种提取方法进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多用于机器翻译，但其输出常包含翻译之外的额外文本（如语言标签、解释、双语重复等"翻译噪声"），该问题普遍存在却缺乏专门基准和系统研究。

Method: 分析12个LLM在22个语言对上超过790,000条翻译输出，识别出12种反复出现的噪声模式，归为格式噪声和内容噪声两类；据此构建TransClean基准（9,900对噪声-干净翻译，含8,800条合成与1,100条人工整理实例）；在基准上评估两种提取方法：基于跨度检测（利用翻译质量估计模型）与基于LLM提示提取。

Result: 通过对大规模输出的分析识别出12种噪声模式并分类；构建了TransClean受控基准，并对两种提取方法进行了评估。

Conclusion: 该基准与分析提供了首个系统框架，用于评估和改善LLM翻译输出的干净程度（cleanliness）。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for machine translation, yet their outputs often contain additional text beyond the translation itself, such as language labels, explanations or bilingual repetitions, which we term translation noise. Despite its prevalence, this problem lacks dedicated benchmarks and systematic study. We analyze over 790,000 translation outputs from 12 LLMs across 22 language pairs (LPs) and identify 12 recurring noise patterns, which we group into formatting and content noise. Building on the observed patterns, we construct TransClean, a controlled benchmark of 9,900 pairs of noisy and clean translation outputs, comprising 8,800 synthetically generated instances and 1,100 manually curated authentic instances. We evaluate two extraction approaches on the TransClean benchmark: 1) a span-based extraction method leveraging translation quality estimation models for span detection, and 2) an LLM-based extraction method that prompts an LLM to isolate the translation. Our benchmark and analysis provide the first systematic framework to evaluate and improve the cleanliness of LLM translation outputs.

</details>


### [116] [Cross-Lingual Clinical Annotation Projection as Constrained Text Generation: A Six-Language Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11450)
*Álvaro Rey-Blanes,Francisco J. Moreno-Barea,Francisco J. Veredas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出将跨语言临床标注投影建模为文本保持的文档级生成任务，用受限LLM直接将实体标签插入目标语言文本，配合确定性验证与字符偏移重建，在6种语言上取得优于候选跨度投影的严格F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在验证跨语言临床标注投影是否可作为文本保持的文档级生成任务，生成可验证的字符级标注，并刻画其鲁棒性与计算权衡。

Method: 开发受限LLM投影流程：将实体标签直接插入不可变的目标语言文本，再做确定性验证和字符偏移重建；与监督式候选跨度投影、混合ML-LLM精炼进行对比。将西班牙语的疾病、症状、检查标注迁移到六种语言，用MultiClinAI金标准评估，采用严格跨度匹配和字符重叠F1。

Result: 直接LLM投影表现最强且最稳定。GLM 5.2在18个语言-实体组合上平均严格F1为0.9201，本地部署的Gemma4:31B为0.9133。最佳配置在全部18个设置上均超越此前SOTA，提升0.0564–0.1512，产出55,416个带重建偏移的接地提及。

Conclusion: 直接基于LLM的投影能实现高质量多语言临床标注迁移，有助于扩展到标注数据稀缺的语言；结合本地推理与确定性验证可显著降低多语言临床语料构建的专家时间和成本。

Abstract: Background: To determine whether cross-lingual clinical annotation projection can be formulated as a text-preserving, document-level generative task that produces verifiable character-level annotations for multilingual clinical corpus construction, and to characterize its robustness and computational trade-offs relative to candidate-based projection pipelines. Methods: We developed a constrained LLM projection workflow that inserts entity tags directly into immutable target-language text, followed by deterministic validation and character-offset reconstruction. We evaluated it alongside supervised candidate-span projection and hybrid ML-LLM refinement for transferring Spanish Disease, Symptom, and Procedure annotations into six languages. Evaluation used MultiClinAI gold standard with strict span matching and character-overlap F1 Results: Direct LLM projection achieved the strongest and most consistent performance. GLM 5.2 obtained a mean Strict F1 of 0.9201 across 18 language-entity combinations, while locally deployable Gemma4:31B achieved 0.9133. The best LLM configuration improved Strict F1 over the previous state of the art in all 18 settings, by 0.0564-0.1512, yielding 55,416 grounded mentions with reconstructed offsets. Conclusions: Direct LLM-based projection enables high-quality multilingual clinical annotation transfer and provides a practical approach for extending clinical NLP resources to languages with fewer annotated datasets and language-specific tools. Combined with local inference and deterministic validation, it can substantially reduce expert time and cost for multilingual clinical corpus construction.

</details>


### [117] [Structural priors for data-efficient language learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11505)
*Yana Veitsman,Jonas Mayer Martins,Jonathan Lautenschlager,Lisa Beinborn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究非语言数据（音乐、概率语法、元胞自动机）预训练能否为语言建模提供有用先验。这些迁移能降低语言建模损失并减小权重偏移，但不稳定地改善下游语言任务，且效率低于直接用语言数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效语言学习需要减少对大规模数据和计算资源的依赖，因此探索用非语言数据预训练以获得对自然语言有用的先验（作为多语言建模的权重初始化）。

Method: 在非语言数据上训练模型，再迁移到语言建模；通过下一词预测损失、模型权重偏移和下游语言基准进行评估。

Result: 音乐、概率语法和元胞自动机等符号数据比随机初始化产生更低的语言建模损失，且伴随更小的权重偏移，表明模型处于更优参数区域；但更低损失并未稳定转化为更好的下游语言表现，非语言迁移效率低于额外语言数据。

Conclusion: 非语言数据可作为下一词预测训练目标的部分替代，但不能可靠支持更广泛的语言泛化。

Abstract: Efficient language learning requires methods to reduce the reliance on large data and computational resources. We investigate structural transfer: First training models on non-language data to induce useful priors for natural language. This approach is a form of weight initialization for multilingual language modeling. We evaluate transfer via next-token-prediction loss, weight shifts in the model, and downstream linguistic benchmarks. Several symbolic data types - notably music, probabilistic grammars, and cellular automata - yield lower language-modeling loss than random initialization. These gains coincide with smaller weight shifts during subsequent language training, suggesting that structural transfer positions models in a more favorable region of the parameter space. However, a lower loss does not translate consistently into better downstream linguistic performance, and transfer from non-language data is less efficient than additional language data. We conclude that non-language data can serve as a partial substitute for language data for the training objective of next-token prediction but does not reliably support broader linguistic generalization.

</details>


### [118] [Complex-Text Robustness Evaluation and Failure Diagnosis for Low-Resource Multilingual Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11545)
*Tianlun Zuo,Ziyu Zhang,Tingzhi Mao,Zhonghua Fu,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出面向低资源多语言TTS的复杂文本鲁棒性诊断框架，从内容一致性、语言一致性、生成稳定性三维度评估，并设计TRS轻量文本风险分数进行合成前风险预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低资源多语言TTS评估多聚焦自然度、说话人相似度和常规句子的内容一致性，对复杂文本输入（数字、日期、命名实体、长句、语码转换、标点结构）下的失效模式诊断不足。

Method: 构建三维鲁棒性诊断框架（内容一致性、语言一致性、生成稳定性）；针对泰语、越南语、斯瓦希里语、印尼语设计多语言鲁棒性测试方案；引入自动诊断指标（字符错误率CER、语种识别准确率、时长异常率）；提出无需标注与训练的轻量文本风险分数TRS。

Result: 在OmniVoice、VoxCPM2、MMS-TTS三系统上实验表明，复杂文本暴露出常规短句评估无法反映的系统性失败模式；各系统在数字规范化、命名实体处理、长文本生成和语码转换上脆弱点各异；TRS与内容错误和时长异常呈正相关。

Conclusion: TRS可作为低成本、合成前即用的复杂文本风险诊断指标，为低资源多语言TTS的鲁棒性评估提供新视角与实用工具。

Abstract: Low-resource multilingual text-to-speech (TTS) systems have expanded language coverage, but their robustness under complex text inputs remains insufficiently diagnosed. Existing evaluations mainly focus on naturalness, speaker similarity, and content consistency using regular test sentences, while providing limited insight into how multilingual TTS systems fail when handling challenging inputs such as numbers, dates, named entities, long sentences, code-switched expressions, and punctuation-related structures. This paper proposes a complex-text robustness diagnosis framework for low-resource multilingual TTS. We evaluate robustness from three dimensions: content consistency, language consistency, and generation stability. A multilingual robustness testing scheme is designed for Thai, Vietnamese, Swahili, and Indonesian, covering ordinary sentences and multiple types of complex text inputs. We further introduce automatic diagnostic metrics, including character error rate, language identification accuracy, and duration abnormal rate. To support input-level risk analysis before speech generation, we propose a lightweight Text Risk Score (TRS), which estimates synthesis risk from interpretable text features without manual annotation or model training. Experiments on three representative multilingual TTS systems, including OmniVoice, VoxCPM2, and MMS-TTS, show that complex text inputs expose systematic failure patterns that are not fully reflected by ordinary short-sentence evaluation. Different systems exhibit distinct vulnerabilities in number normalization, named entity handling, long-text generation, and code-switched input processing. Furthermore, TRS shows a positive correlation with content errors and duration abnormalities, demonstrating its usefulness as a low-cost pre-synthesis indicator for complex-text risk diagnosis in low-resource multilingual TTS.

</details>


### [119] [A Training-Free, Alignment-Free Approach to Corporate Intelligence: Application to SEC Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11620)
*Jean-François Delpech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练、免对齐的稀疏种子向量框架，通过将词串哈希到固定高维基，使所有文档与时间节点天然处于同一坐标系，实现高效的企业披露分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维稠密文本嵌入与大语言模型在金融披露分析中面临上下文窗口限制、幻觉风险、高计算成本，以及不同模型间向量空间任意旋转的问题。

Method: 将词串哈希到固定高维基生成确定性稀疏种子向量，按句上下文累加得到语料特定的语义签名；签名线性可组合，无需训练或对齐，仅用普通CPU完成文档比较、发行人指纹、词汇演变跟踪与主题句抽取。

Result: 在多年SEC文件（10-K、10-Q、8-K）语料上验证，波音737 MAX危机、英特尔供应链中断、邦吉收购Viterra等重大事件呈现为可解释的语义剖面，且可追溯到具体源句。

Conclusion: 该框架无需领域训练与LLM推理，即可低成本、可解释地进行企业情报分析，为金融披露分析提供了实用替代方案。

Abstract: High-dimensional dense text embeddings and large language models face real obstacles in financial-disclosure analysis: context-window limits, hallucination risk, high computational cost, and the arbitrary rotation of vector spaces across independently trained models. We present a training-free, alignment-free framework for corporate intelligence built on deterministic sparse seed vectors. Hashing word strings into a fixed high-dimensional basis places all documents and all temporal epochs in a common coordinate system by construction, removing any need for training or alignment. Accumulating these seed vectors across sentence contexts yields corpus-specific semantic signatures that compose linearly, supporting sub-second document comparison, issuer fingerprinting, tracking of how an issuer's vocabulary shifts between filings, and thematic sentence extraction, all on ordinary CPU hardware. Demonstrating the approach on a multi-year corpus of SEC filings (10-K, 10-Q, 8-K), we show how material corporate events, among them Boeing's 737 MAX crisis, Intel's supply-chain disruptions, and Bunge's acquisition of Viterra, emerge as distinct, interpretable semantic profiles, each traceable to the exact source sentences that produced it, with no domain-specific training and no LLM inference.

</details>


### [120] [Structured Transforms for Low-Overhead Quantization of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11687)
*Daria Cherniuk,Alexander Rudikov,Boris Kashin,Ivan Oseledets*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于Kashin分解的改进权重量化算法，用符号随机DCT替代稠密随机正交矩阵，实现O(N log N)复杂度，稳定支持2-bit聚类，在4-bit及压力场景下与OPTQ/QuIP等竞争且数值稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前Kashin-decomposition权重量化依赖稠密随机正交矩阵，单次迭代代价O(N^2)，且需要多次重启k-means来确定聚类中心，成为瓶颈。同时QuIP系列在压力配置下会出现发散或NaN，需要更稳定、更高效的量化方法。

Method: 保留将权重分解为两个分量（一个有界无穷范数，另一个正交变换后有界无穷范数）的核心分解，但用符号随机离散余弦变换(DCT)替代稠密随机正交矩阵，将每次迭代成本降至O(N log N)。采用带交替更新的贪心算法，保证每个因子稳定2-bit聚类所需的四峰分布，并提供聚类中心的闭式初始化，消除多重启k-means瓶颈。再与OPTQ式序列误差补偿和QuIP式非相干预处理组合成JAX流水线。

Result: 在OPT、Llama-2和Pythia上按每通道4-bit量化，该方法与OPTQ、QuIP、QuIP-RG及无微调和无矢量量化的QuIP#变体竞争，且wall-clock扩展性更优。在压力配置下（Pythia-6.9B使QuIP变体发散到四位数困惑度、Mistral-7B在LDL回代中出现NaN而中止），Kashin-DCT保持数值稳定并接近FP16基线。推理时每个权重分解为每通道两个2-bit因子码，结构上适合原生2-bit硬件。

Conclusion: Kashin-DCT通过结构化高效的正交变换改进权重分解量化，兼顾效率、收敛性和数值稳定性，在4-bit量化及困难场景中表现稳健，并为原生2-bit硬件推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We revisit Kashin-decomposition-based weight quantization for large language models and propose an improved algorithm with stronger convergence properties and structured, efficient orthogonal transforms. The method retains the core factorization of each weight into two components -- one with bounded infinity norm and the other with bounded infinity norm after an orthogonal transformation -- but replaces the dense random orthogonal matrix with a sign-randomized Discrete Cosine Transform (DCT), reducing the per-iteration cost from $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ to $\mathcal{O}(N \log N)$. The proposed greedy algorithm with alternating updates guarantees the four-peak distribution required for stable 2-bit clustering of each factor and admits closed-form initialization of cluster centers, removing the multi-restart k-means bottleneck of prior work. Composed with OPTQ-style sequential error compensation and QuIP-style incoherence preprocessing, the resulting JAX pipeline is competitive with OPTQ, QuIP, QuIP-RG and a fine-tuning- and vector-quantization-free variant of QuIP# at 4-bit per channel on OPT, Llama-2 and Pythia, with favorable wall-clock scaling. The bounded-$\ell_\infty$ factorization is also notably robust: on stress configurations where QuIP variants diverge to four-digit perplexity (Pythia-6.9B) or abort with NaNs in LDL back-substitution (Mistral-7B), Kashin-DCT remains numerically stable and stays close to FP16 baseline. At inference time, each weight decomposes into two 2-bit factor codes per channel that are structurally suited to native-2-bit hardware.

</details>


### [121] [Negative Self-Distillation: Learning to Reason by Avoiding Flaws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11699)
*Rongcan Pei,Zhepei Wei,Shuyao Xu,Xinyu Zhu,Wei-Lin Chen,Yu Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Negative Self-Distillation (NSD)，通过让模型远离自生成的"负面"推理轨迹（而非模仿特权信息），并配合动态门控机制隔离推理关键token，从而提升LLM复杂推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) 让模型用真值等特权信息做自己的老师，但强制模仿"过度自信"的推理轨迹会抑制不确定性表达与探索性、自我纠错行为，导致复杂推理任务性能严重下降。

Method: NSD不依赖真值或外部监督，而是让模型自生成针对问题的负面条件（如充当"粗心推理者"），将学生分布推离该自生成负面教师。为解决朴素去学习目标会把缺陷推理token与基础语言token混淆的问题，设计了动态门控机制，自动识别并隔离推理关键token，使梯度更新只针对行为缺陷，保留语言先验。

Result: 实验表明，NSD持续优于OPSD以及其它无标签、自举式强化学习基线。

Conclusion: NSD提供了一种通过"远离错误"而非"模仿特权解"来优化LLM的新框架，在保持语言能力的同时改善了复杂推理表现。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a popular paradigm for large language model (LLM) self-improvement, allowing models to act as their own teachers by leveraging privileged information such as ground-truth solutions. However, recent findings indicate that OPSD can severely degrade the performance of LLMs on complex reasoning tasks: By forcing the student to imitate an artificially confident reasoning trace conditioned on privileged information, OPSD inadvertently suppresses expressions of uncertainty and penalizes the exploratory, self-corrective behaviors required to solve challenging problems. To address this, we introduce Negative Self-Distillation (NSD), a new framework that optimizes LLMs by diverging from flawed reasoning rather than imitating privileged solutions. Instead of relying on ground-truth answers or external supervision, NSD uses the model itself to generate a question-specific negative condition (eg, acting as a ``careless reasoner'') and pushes the student's distribution away from this self-generated negative teacher. Naively applying unlearning objectives to achieve this divergence is problematic, as flawed reasoning tokens are confounded with basic linguistic tokens; indiscriminately penalizing both risks catastrophically degrading the model's foundational language capabilities. We resolve this by designing a dynamic gating mechanism that automatically identifies and isolates reasoning-critical tokens, ensuring gradient updates target only behavioral flaws while preserving the model's linguistic priors. Empirically, NSD consistently outperforms OPSD and other label-free, self-bootstrapping reinforcement learning (RL) baselines.

</details>


### [122] [The Eloquence submission for Task 2 of the Interspeech 2026 MLC-SLM challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11724)
*Jordi Luque,Lorenzo Concina,Marco Matassoni,Alessio Brutti,Filippo Vella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍Eloquence团队针对Interspeech 2026 MLC-SLM挑战赛Task 2（21种语言的多语言多选题问答）的三种方法，最佳方案达到0.81宏准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多语言多选题问答（MCQA）任务，并针对模型存在的标签偏差问题提出改进方案。

Method: 三种方法：1) 用LoRA微调Voxtral-Mini-3B，结合跨语言数据增强、ASR转录增强和时间戳感知音频裁剪；2) 对冻结的Voxtral-24B进行多模态上下文学习以纠正标签偏差；3) 基于三层语音锚定记忆（声学身份、语义内容、知识图谱）的免训练检索系统。

Result: 方法一：Phase 2宏准确率0.72；方法二：0.81（最佳）；方法三：0.68。三者均显著优于官方基线。

Conclusion: 多模态ICL方法表现最佳，证明纠正标签偏差的重要性；三种方法均超越官方基线。

Abstract: This paper details the Eloquence team's approach to Task 2 of the 2nd MLC-SLM challenge at Interspeech 2026, which involves multilingual Multiple-Choice Question Answering (MCQA) across 21 languages. Three approaches are explored. First, we fine-tune Voxtral-Mini-3B via LoRA with cross-lingual data augmentation, ASR transcript augmentation and timestamp-aware audio cropping, achieving 0.72 macro-accuracy on evaluation Phase 2. Second, we apply multimodal in-context learning (ICL) to the frozen Voxtral-24B model to correct a strong label bias, reaching 0.81, our best result. Third, a training-free retrieval system based on a three-layer voice-anchored memory combining acoustic identity, semantic content, and a knowledge graph achieves 0.68. All three systems substantially outperform the official baseline.

</details>


### [123] [LOCUS: Task-Aware Low-Rank Post-Training for Token-Efficient Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11739)
*Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LOCUS，通过选择任务感知的低秩子空间来最小化输出token成本，在不改变偏好对齐目标的情况下减少生成冗长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型服务成本与输出长度成正比，但标准偏好对齐常增加回复冗长却不提升实用性。作者研究后训练更新的参数化方式是否影响生成长度。

Method: LOCUS选择任务感知的低秩适配子空间，在效用约束下最小化输出token成本。子空间内保持原生偏好目标，主干网络冻结，仅更新极少量参数。

Result: 在HH-RLHF对话偏好上，对Pythia-2.8B和Qwen2.5-3B进行评估。LOCUS将续写长度分别最多降低39.84%和14.87–17.58%，仅更新0.24–0.28%参数，内部偏好诊断无实质变化。

Conclusion: 后训练更新的参数化方式可有效控制生成长度，LOCUS在保持对齐质量的同时显著降低输出成本。

Abstract: Large language model serving costs scale directly with output sequence length, yet standard preference alignment often inflates response verbosity without improving utility. We study whether the parameterization of post-training updates affects generation length: low-rank subspaces alter sequence length without modifying the alignment loss. We present LOCUS, a method that selects a task-aware low-rank adaptation subspace to minimize output-token cost subject to a utility constraint. Within this subspace, post-training retains the native preference objective with a frozen backbone. Across Anthropic HH-RLHF dialogue preferences, we evaluate two $\sim$3B decoder backbones, Pythia-2.8B and Qwen2.5-3B, against protocol-matched full-parameter DPO and DrDPO branches and the released SamPO checkpoint. LOCUS reduces continuation length by up to 39.84\% on Pythia-2.8B and by 14.87--17.58\% on Qwen2.5-3B while updating only 0.24--0.28\% of model parameters, with no material change in the internal preference diagnostic.

</details>


### [124] [Component-Aware Differential Privacy for Federated Multilingual Speech-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11762)
*Jordi Luque,Fernando López,Aleix Sant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现逐层差分隐私裁剪在语音大模型上因编码器与语言解码器更新范数差异巨大而失效（跨组件预算崩塌），提出双池分配的 $α$-split 方法恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐层DP裁剪按参数量分配裁剪预算以提升联邦学习梯度保真度，但当语音LLM的声学编码器与语言解码器更新范数相差一个数量级时，该方案失效；单池逐层方法出现跨组件预算崩塌，导致WER恶化甚至训练崩溃，需解决此不平衡问题。

Method: 在六种逐层方法与三种语音LLM架构上经验性诊断根因；提出 $α$-split 双池分配方法，将编码器与LLM参数归一化到独立池中，并证明联合 $\ell_2$ 敏感度与原始 $(\varepsilon,\delta)$-DP 保证不变；在架构校准的 $α$ 下进行实验。

Result: 单池逐层方法在范数不平衡严重时WER远超平坦全局裁剪或训练崩溃；不平衡较缓时自适应单池方法部分恢复，证明崩塌严重程度随组件间范数比缩放。$α$-split 在架构校准 $α$ 下恢复WER效用，同时以仅 +2.6% 的LLM噪声开销，为编码器提供 4.47 倍更紧的逐组件噪声保护以抵御基于说话人声纹的梯度反演攻击。

Conclusion: 逐层DP裁剪的多池分配需考虑组件间更新范数不平衡；$α$-split在不损失DP保证的前提下兼顾效用与隐私，为语音LLM联邦学习提供更优的隐私-效用权衡。

Abstract: Per-layer differential privacy (DP) clipping improves gradient fidelity in federated learning by allocating per-matrix clipping budgets proportional to parameter count. We show that this recipe breaks for speech large language models (speech-LLMs), when the acoustic encoder and the language decoder differ by an order of magnitude in update norm. Single-pool per-layer methods suffer \emph{cross-component budget collapse}, dragging word error rate (WER) far from flat global clipping or collapsing training entirely. When the norm imbalance is milder, adaptive single-pool methods partially recover, confirming that collapse severity scales with the inter-component norm ratio. We empirically diagnose the root cause across six per-layer methods and three speech-LLM architectures. We then propose \emph{$α$-split}, a two-pool allocation that normalises encoder and LLM parameters into independent pools, and show that joint $\ell_2$ sensitivity and the original $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP guarantee are unchanged. At architecture-calibrated $α$, our method recovers WER utility compared to flat DP, while granting the encoder $4.47{\times}$ tighter per-component noise protection against speaker voice-based gradient-inversion attacks at only $+2.6\%$ LLM noise overhead.

</details>


### [125] [Recognizing Is Not Reversing: A Controlled Inversion Test of Fact-Preserving News Framing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11769)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出受控"框架反转"测试，发现LLM能保持事实但难以撤销已知的框架变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有新闻框架研究多关注生成、检测或重写是否更中立，却无法直接证明模型能否在固定事实的前提下撤销已知的框架变换。

Method: 基于评价性词汇、施事实现、信息显著性三种框架实现，对60篇新闻、3种干预强度构建540个保留原子事实并记录编辑的配对变体，在Qwen、DeepSeek、Kimi上测试。

Result: 事实保持率约0.84，但干预反转率仅0.044–0.068；即使正确识别框架类型与方向，汇总反转率也只有0.071。

Conclusion: 事实忠实、框架识别与框架反转三者存在明显分离：识别文章如何被框架化并不意味着能够撤销该框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to analyze and rewrite news, yet current framing studies mainly evaluate generation, detection, or whether rewritten text appears more neutral. They do not directly show whether a model can undo a known framing transformation while keeping the facts fixed. We introduce a controlled inversion test over three established textual realizations of framing: evaluative lexis, agency realization, and information salience. Across 60 news articles and three intervention strengths, this yields 540 paired variants with preserved atomic facts and recorded edits. Across Qwen, DeepSeek, and Kimi, factual preservation remains near 0.84, whereas intervention reversal is 0.044--0.068. Even when both framing type and direction are recognized correctly, pooled reversal reaches 0.071. These results reveal a clear separation between factual fidelity, framing recognition, and framing inversion: recognizing how an article is framed does not imply that the framing can be undone.

</details>


### [126] [The widening evaluation gap in medical large language model research 2023 to 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11770)
*Raad Bin Tareaf,Murad Al-Rajab,Samia Loucif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估医学AI研究是否跟得上被评估的大模型迭代速度：PubMed 2023.1–2026.6共11,628篇、覆盖14个临床领域，但仅2.5%采用随机/对照/前瞻设计，评估滞后从1.33增至6.08个季度；模型迁移仅抵消56%的"过时漂移"，随机试验所评估模型更旧，严格性与时效性存在张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型每隔几个季度就被新版本取代，而临床证据产出需数年。作者担忧医学研究无法跟上其所评估系统的迭代速度，因此系统考察"评估滞后"问题。

Method: 检索PubMed 2023年1月–2026年6月、14个临床领域共11,628条记录；统计研究设计类型（随机、对照、前瞻）比例；计算评估滞后（从被点名最新模型发布到研究发表的时间）随时间变化；以固定模型构成为反事实基准，量化向新系统迁移对"过时漂移"的抵消程度；比较不同设计间所评估模型的年龄。

Result: 文献量增长45倍，但仅2.5%采用随机/对照/前瞻设计。评估滞后由1.33增至6.08个季度；模型迁移仅抵消56%的漂移（95% CI 50–65）。随机试验所评估模型中位年龄比其他设计旧4.6个季度（P=3×10⁻¹⁹）；但在仍处于开发中的模型研究中，各设计无差异；62%的随机试验评估的是已停用的模型家族。

Conclusion: 严格性与时效性之间存在张力，而这种张力源于模型选择而非研究时间线本身。

Abstract: Large language models are superseded every few quarters; clinical evidence takes years. We asked whether medical research is keeping pace with the systems it evaluates. PubMed returned 11,628 records for January 2023 to June 2026 across fourteen clinical domains, growing 45-fold; 2.5% used a randomised, controlled or prospective design. Evaluation lag, from a study's newest named model release to its own publication, widened from 1.33 to 6.08 quarters. Because discontinued models age mechanically, we benchmarked this against a counterfactual holding model composition fixed: migration to newer systems offset only 56% of the drift (95% CI 50-65). Randomised trials evaluated models a median 4.6 quarters older than other designs (P = 3 x 10^-19), yet among studies naming a model still under development no design differed from any other; 62% of randomised trials evaluated a discontinued family. Rigour and currency are in tension, and that tension reflects model selection rather than research timelines.

</details>


### [127] [Beyond Word Error Rate: A Switch Aware Evaluation of ASR and Audio Language Models on English Yoruba Code-Switched Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11786)
*Chibuzor Okocha,Christan Earl Grant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对11个现代语音系统在英语-约鲁巴语码切换语音上进行切换感知评估，发现聚合WER掩盖了码切换行为，约鲁巴语识别严重崩溃，错误集中在切换点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR和音频语言模型在单语基准上错误率低，但在低资源、含变音符号语言的码切换语音上表现缺乏刻画，需要切换感知的评估方法。

Method: 使用确定性2000句评估集和统一评分流程，评估6个ASR模型和5个音频LM。除WER外，提出切换进入词错误率(SETER)、窗口化切换点错误率、语言特定错误率和忽略变音符号的WER等诊断指标。

Result: 聚合WER掩盖码切换行为：WER最优的ASR模型与领先音频LM在WER上无统计差异，但后者在所有切换局部指标上显著更优。忠实系统中约鲁巴语token错误率约0.97，英语远好，错误集中于切换到约鲁巴语处。部分生成式音频LM无法作精确转写，出现翻译、冗长和提示泄漏，且强依赖提示。

Conclusion: 聚合WER不足以评估码切换语音，需采用切换感知指标。作者发布清单、指标实现和评估脚本以支持非洲码切换语音的可复现基准测试。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems and audio language models (audio LMs) now report low error rates on monolingual benchmarks, but their behavior on code switched speech in low resource, diacritic rich languages remains poorly characterized. We present a switch aware evaluation of eleven modern systems (six ASR models and five audio LMs) on English Yoruba code-switched speech, using a deterministic 2000 utterance evaluation set and a shared scoring pipeline. Beyond word error rate (WER), we report switch localized diagnostics: a switch entry token error rate (SETER), windowed switch point error rates, language specific error rates, and a diacritic insensitive WER. Our central finding is that aggregate WER hides code switching behavior. The best system by WER (an ASR model) is statistically indistinguishable from a leading audio LM on WER, yet the audio LM is significantly better on every switch localized metric. Across faithful systems, Yoruba token recognition collapses (error 0.97 for almost all systems) while English tokens are recognized far better, and errors concentrate sharply at switches into Yoruba. Several generative audio LMs fail as exact transcribers, producing translation, verbosity, and prompt leakage that are strongly prompt dependent. We release manifests, metric implementations, and evaluation scripts to support reproducible, switch aware benchmarking for African code switched speech.

</details>


### [128] [Target leakage, not model class, explains reported accuracy in survey-based cardiovascular screening: a leakage-tiered audit of glass-box and tabular foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11838)
*Raad Bin Tareaf,Murad Al-Rajab,Samia Loucif,Samer Ellaham,Cedric Schmitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文检验心血管筛查模型的高AUROC是否源于目标泄漏，发现去除两个诊断后特征即让所有模型丧失约0.05 AUROC，模型间差距缩至0.0045；透明模型性能不劣且快约104倍，评估实践而非模型容量才是瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家健康调查训练的心血管筛查模型AUROC普遍接近0.89，作者质疑这一精度究竟反映真实学习还是目标泄漏，以及表格基础模型能否改变结论、部署所需的各项属性是否经得住联合检验。

Method: 在2022年BRFSS的442,067名受访者上，对心肌梗死患病情况基准测试十种分类器（线性、树集成、神经网络、玻璃盒、表格基础模型），按泄漏风险递减设五个特征层级；逐一审计区分度、校准、显式阈值下公平性、保形覆盖、解释忠实度与推理成本，再以冻结的模型和阈值应用于2023年的430,755名受访者。

Result: 去除两个诊断后特征使每个模型损失0.049-0.051 AUROC，全场压缩到0.0045宽带内；玻璃盒可解释提升机在规定0.005边际内不劣于所有替代方案，且评分速度约为最强基础模型的104倍。单一阈值检出女性心梗75.4%、男性89.0%，编辑模型形状函数将差距降至0.010；边际保形预测对男性覆盖0.86、60岁以上成人0.82，Mondrian校准修复各层；冻结模型迁移AUROC差异在0.002以内。

Conclusion: 该领域报告的性能余量属于特征集而非学习器，透明性无可测代价且使公平修复与不确定性条件化可直接审计，评估实践而非模型容量才是真正的约束。

Abstract: Cardiovascular screening models trained on national health surveys routinely report areas under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) near 0.89. We asked whether that accuracy reflects learning or target leakage, whether tabular foundation models change the answer, and whether the properties deployment requires survive joint examination. We benchmarked ten classifiers spanning linear, tree-ensemble, neural, glass-box, and tabular foundation classes for prevalent myocardial infarction in 442,067 respondents of the 2022 Behavioral Risk Factor Surveillance System across five feature tiers of decreasing leakage risk. Each was audited for discrimination, calibration, fairness at an explicit screening threshold, conformal coverage, explanation faithfulness, and inference cost, then applied -- models and thresholds frozen -- to 430,755 respondents of 2023. Removing two post-diagnostic features cost every model 0.049-0.051 AUROC, collapsing the field into a 0.0045-wide band. The glass-box explainable boosting machine was non-inferior to every alternative within a pre-specified 0.005 margin while scoring the cohort roughly 104 times faster than the strongest foundation model. One threshold detected 75.4% of women's infarctions against 89.0% of men's; editing the model's shape functions reduced the gap to 0.010. Marginal conformal prediction gave 0.86 coverage to men and 0.82 to adults over 60; Mondrian calibration repaired every stratum. Frozen models transported within 0.002 AUROC. Reported headroom in this literature is a property of the feature set, not the learner. Transparency cost nothing measurable and made fairness repair and uncertainty conditioning directly auditable. Evaluation practice, not model capacity, is the binding constraint.

</details>


### [129] [IndicTriMix: Developing Language Identification Datasets and Models for Tri-Language Code-Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11851)
*Pruthwik Mishra,Rudra Trivedi,Avi Patel,Ashok Urlana,Shrikant Malviya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究代码混合文本中的词级语言识别，将其建模为序列标注任务，微调MuRIL和XLM-RoBERTa，在印地语、古吉拉特语和孟加拉语三种配置上评估，并发布基准、生成方法和微调模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体中用户经常在同一句话内切换多种语言，语言识别对代码混合文本处理至关重要；而面向单语文本的传统模型不适合代码混合场景下的词级语言识别。

Method: 将任务形式化为序列标注问题，微调适合印度语言的上下文Transformer模型MuRIL和XLM-RoBERTa；提出两种利用三种语言平行句生成代码混合文本的方法。

Result: 在印地语、古吉拉特语和孟加拉语三种数据配置上进行评估，预测每个token的语言标签；发布人工标注测试集的基准，并公开微调模型。实验证明上下文嵌入对多语言社交媒体文本的词级语言识别有效。

Conclusion: 上下文Transformer模型能有效完成代码混合文本的词级语言识别；作者公开基准与微调模型以促进可复现性和未来研究。

Abstract: Language identification in code-mixed text, largely observed in social media, is highly essential when users frequently switch between multiple languages within a single utterance. Accurately identifying the languages of code-mixed tokens becomes an urgent necessity. Traditional language identification models, designed for monolingual text, are not well suited for token-level language identification in code-mixed settings. We formulate the task as a sequence labeling problem and fine-tune contextual transformer-based models MuRIL and XLM-RoBERTa best suited for Indian languages. We evaluate these systems on three different data configurations (Hindi, Gujarati, and Bengali) to predict language labels for individual tokens. We release a benchmark for language identification in code-mixed tokens with manually annotated test sets. We propose two approaches of code-mixed generation using parallel sentences of three languages. The trained models demonstrate the effectiveness of contextual embeddings for token-level language identification in multilingual social media text. For reproducibility and to facilitate future research, we publicly release our fine-tuned models.

</details>


### [130] [Epistemic orientation predicts legislative effectiveness among members of the US Congress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11865)
*Segun Aroyehun,Stephan Lewandowsky,David Garcia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究将证据导向语言分析从国会整体转向议员个人，使用EMI评分衡量议员演讲和推文中证据与直觉语言的相对比例。结果显示：意识形态越极端的议员在国会发言中使用更少证据导向语言；EMI在演讲和推特间具跨平台一致性；EMI与议员立法效能（LES）正相关。表明证据导向语言是个体层面立法沟通与效能的有意义属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真相与证据导向的沟通是民主治理、问责和集体决策的重要基础。既有研究显示，自1970年代中期以来美国国会议场演讲中的证据导向语言有所下降，但这多停留在国会整体（session）层面。本文希望将分析从国会会议转向议员个人，检验认识论取向（epistemic orientation）是否在议员间存在系统性差异，以及它是否与政治行为和立法效能相关。

Method: 使用Evidence-Minus-Intuition（EMI）评分，测量国会议场演讲和Twitter推文中证据导向相对直觉导向语言的比例。将这些指标与议员层面的数据（意识形态、机构职位、沟通情境、立法效能评分LES）相链接进行分析。

Result: 1）意识形态越极端的议员在国会议场使用越少的证据导向语言；2）EMI具跨平台一致性——在演讲中更证据导向的议员在Twitter上也更证据导向，但Twitter整体EMI较低；3）国会议场演讲中的EMI与个人立法效能正相关，且在控制意识形态及大量政治、机构、人口、议题和沟通量变量后仍显著。

Conclusion: 证据导向语言不仅是国会话语的总体特征，也是议员个体层面立法沟通与立法效能的有意义属性。

Abstract: Truth and evidence-based communication provide important foundations for democratic governance, accountability, and collective decision-making. Prior work shows that evidence-oriented language in US congressional floor speeches has declined since the mid-1970s, alongside broader changes in legislative productivity and polarization. This study shifts the analysis from congressional sessions to individual members of Congress to examine whether epistemic orientation varies systematically across legislators and whether it relates to political behavior and legislative effectiveness. Using the Evidence-Minus-Intuition (EMI) score, we measure the relative prevalence of evidence-oriented versus intuition-oriented language in congressional floor speeches and Twitter posts. We link these measures to legislator-level data on ideology, institutional position, communication context, and Legislative Effectiveness Score (LES). The results show that more ideologically extreme members use less evidence-oriented language on the congressional floor. EMI also exhibits cross-platform consistency with members who use more evidence-oriented language in floor speeches also being more evidence-oriented on Twitter, although EMI is lower on Twitter overall. Finally, EMI in congressional speeches is positively associated with individual legislative effectiveness, even after accounting for ideology and extensive political, institutional, demographic, topical, and communication volume controls. These findings suggest that evidence-oriented language is not only an aggregate feature of congressional discourse but also a meaningful attribute of individual-level legislative communication and effectiveness.

</details>


### [131] [Augustinian BabyLM: What Ostensive Definition Can and Cannot Teach a Small Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11870)
*Lisa Bylinina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文让小型掩码语言模型(DeBERTa)在训练前对视觉可定位词使用图像区域派生的嵌入、其余词随机初始化，检验"以指物方式学词义"能否给语言模型留下持久印记。视觉初始化在BabyLM抽象语法任务上几乎无影响，但对物体属性知识(COMPS及自建的Visual-Property Swap)在种子词上有稳定、可复现的优势，且合成接地可因果性地转移该优势。功能词和抽象词也保留视觉种子并降低掩码预测损失，但无基准能检出这一效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文受圣奥古斯丁"通过指物(ostension)学词义"的图景启发，想知道是否可以让语言模型不从一开始就用随机词嵌入，而是为视觉可定位的词预先注入来自图像区域的语义；并检验这种视觉接地对不同类型语言知识(抽象语法、物体属性、功能词/抽象词)的影响，以及现有基准能否捕捉其作用。

Method: 用10M词训练小型掩码语言模型DeBERTa；训练前，视觉接地token的嵌入来自其所标注的图像区域，其他token随机初始化；在BabyLM基准上评估抽象语法知识，在COMPS上做零样本评估，并构建语料定制的Visual-Property Swap基准(颜色、材质、尺寸、形状，带逐项训练频率与是否被seed的标注)；还通过合成接地此前未seed的词，做因果检验；并测held-out掩码预测损失。

Result: 视觉初始化留下可测的印记并持续到训练结束；在多数BabyLM基准上不可见，即不影响抽象语法表现；COMPS是唯一零样本例外，所有配置下seeding均有帮助；在Visual-Property Swap上，视觉seed模型有持续且可复现的优势，且局限于被seed的词；对未seed词做合成接地可把优势因果性地转移到这些词；功能词与抽象词同样获得强视觉种子并在训练中保留，held-out掩码预测损失在每个种子配置下都下降，但没有基准能记录此效应。

Conclusion: 视觉接地能以指物方式为语言模型提供持久、可因果验证的词义优势，尤其体现在物体属性知识上，但对抽象语法影响甚微；功能词和抽象词的收益虽存在于训练目标中却未被现有基准检出，说明如何评估这类效应仍是开放问题。

Abstract: A language model normally begins training with random word embeddings: whatever 'banana' means must be learned from training corpora. I implement St. Augustine's picture of word learning, meaning by ostension, for a small masked language model (DeBERTa) trained on 10M words: before training, visually grounded tokens receive embeddings derived from the image regions they label; other tokens start random. Visual initialization leaves a measurable imprint that lasts until the end of training. At the same time, the effect remains invisible under most BabyLM benchmarks, which probe abstract grammatical knowledge: visual initialization does not affect performance there. The only zero-shot exception is object-property knowledge (COMPS, Misra et al. 2023), where seeding helps in every configuration. To follow up on this result, I build a corpus-tailored version of the Visual-Property Swap benchmark (Lin et al., 2026), which tests color, material, size, and shape knowledge, with per-item training frequency and seeded status. Here, vision-seeded models have a persistent, seed- replicated advantage, confined to the seeded words. As a causal test, I show that synthetic grounding of previously unseeded words transfers the advantage to exactly those words. Function words and abstract vocabulary also receive strong visual seeds and retain them throughout training, and the training objective draws on them: held-out mask-prediction loss falls for these words in every seed. However, no benchmark I run registers this. What evaluation would pick this up remains an open question.

</details>


### [132] [Domain-Specific Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11878)
*Varun Teja Chundru,Debasmita Biswas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个多信号幻觉检测流水线（DeBERTa-v3分类 + MC Dropout不确定性 + 温度标定），在HaluEval上达F1=0.915、AUROC=0.977，并用DPO将生成器幻觉率从85.5%降至37.7%；跨域SciFact实验表明领域专用微调（PubMedBERT）效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的流畅文本可能包含不忠实于事实的陈述（幻觉），需要可靠的响应级幻觉检测方法，并进一步降低生成器的幻觉率。

Method: 多信号检测流水线：微调DeBERTa-v3分类 + Monte Carlo Dropout不确定性量化 + 温度标定；并做上下文消融、学习曲线分析，以及用DPO微调Qwen2.5-0.5B生成器；跨域在SciFact上用PubMedBERT微调。

Result: HaluEval上F1=0.915、AUROC=0.977，各任务F1为QA 0.97、摘要0.96、对话0.82；MC Dropout提升准确率至93.2%。去除知识上下文使摘要F1降24%，表明模型进行真实蕴含推理。25%训练数据即达77%全量性能。DPO使幻觉率从85.5%降至37.7%（相对降55.9%）。跨域SciFact泛化差（F1=0.52），PubMedBERT微调达F1=0.63、AUROC=0.81。

Conclusion: 多信号检测流水线在通用域幻觉检测上表现优异且具备真实推理能力；领域匹配的预训练是跨域适配的最佳策略。

Abstract: Large language models generate fluent text that can contain unfaithful claims -- a phenomenon known as hallucination. We present a multi-signal detection pipeline combining fine-tuned DeBERTa-v3 classification, Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout uncertainty quantification, and temperature-scaled calibration for response-level hallucination detection. Evaluated on the HaluEval benchmark, our pipeline achieves F1=0.915 and AUROC=0.977 on general-domain tasks, with per-task F1 scores of 0.97 (QA), 0.96 (Summarization), and 0.82 (Dialogue). MC Dropout inference further improves accuracy to 93.2%. A context ablation study confirms the model performs genuine entailment reasoning rather than exploiting surface patterns, with summarization F1 dropping 24% when knowledge context is removed. Learning curve analysis reveals that 25% of training data captures 77% of full-data performance. Beyond detection, we apply Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to a Qwen2.5-0.5B generator, reducing its hallucination rate from 85.5% to 37.7% (55.9% relative reduction) as measured by our detector. Cross-domain evaluation on the SciFact biomedical benchmark shows that general-domain training transfers poorly (F1=0.52), motivating domain-specific fine-tuning. PubMedBERT fine-tuned on SciFact achieves F1=0.63 and AUROC=0.81, demonstrating that domain-matched pre-training is the strongest adaptation strategy. Code and models are available at https://github.com/varunteja99/hallucination-detection-nlp

</details>


### [133] [Nuha-Speech: Building General-Purpose Arabic Speech-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11892)
*Yingzhi Wang,Reem Alhazzani,Muhammad Alqurishi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Nuha-Speech项目，构建150万+阿拉伯语语音问答语料，基于Qwen-Omni微调，并设计评估框架，推动阿拉伯语语音大模型基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语音大模型多语言化，阿拉伯语显著 underrepresented，缺乏专门的训练与评估基础设施。

Method: 构建大规模阿拉伯语语音问答语料（150万+样本）用于指令微调；基于不同规模的Qwen-Omni变体进行监督微调；设计含多样任务与定制指标的评估框架。

Result: 建立了涵盖数据、训练、评估的阿拉伯语语音大模型基础设施，并完成多尺度模型微调与系统评估。

Conclusion: 在阿拉伯语语音资源受限条件下，为通用阿拉伯语语音大模型奠定基础设施基础。

Abstract: As Speech Large Language Models (speech-LLMs) become increasingly multilingual, Arabic remains significantly underrepresented, highlighting the need for dedicated infrastructure to train and evaluate Arabic speech-LLMs.
  To address this gap, we introduce Nuha-Speech, a comprehensive initiative to develop general-purpose Arabic speech-LLMs spanning dataset construction, model training, and systematic evaluation. Specifically, we constructed a large-scale Arabic Speech Question-Answering (SQA) corpus comprising over 1.5 million training samples to allow instruction tuning over a broad range of core speech tasks. Then, the corpus was used for supervised fine-tuning based on Qwen-Omni model variants at different scales. Finally, we designed an evaluation framework featuring diverse tasks and tailored metrics. Through this work, we aim to establish foundational infrastructures for Arabic Speech-LLMs under constraints imposed by limited Arabic speech resources.

</details>


### [134] [Distance generalization in transformers: why bother with positional encoding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11913)
*Daniel Henrik Nevermann,Claudius Gros*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer在固定上下文下对token间距离变化的距离泛化能力，构建两种延迟复制任务，考察位置编码方案、数据多样性与距离迁移学习的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长度泛化研究多关注上下文变长，但token间距离改变而上下文长度不变时的距离泛化机制尚不清楚，需深入理解其内在机理。

Method: 构建两个合成延迟复制任务（源与回忆间存在有限距离，token全复制或选择性复制），在训练未见延迟上测试模型，比较RoPE、ALiBi与NoPE，并研究训练中距离数量（数据多样性）及距离迁移学习的影响。

Result: 进行了系统研究，回答了三个问题：位置编码是否提升距离分辨率、数据多样性如何影响性能、距离迁移学习何时为正或负，并强调理解底层机制的重要性。

Conclusion: 距离泛化的关键机制仍待深入理解，位置编码、数据多样性与迁移学习的效果需结合具体机制分析。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution length generalization, namely to extrapolate a task from short to longer context, has been studied intensively for transformers. Here we focus on distance generalization, which probes performance when inter-token distances are changed between training and inference, while keeping a fixed context length. We construct two synthetic delay copy tasks, both involving finite distances between source and recall, where tokens are copied either fully or selectively, and test models on delays unseen during training. We address three questions: (A) Do positional encoding schemes such as RoPE and ALiBi improve distance resolution relative to no positional encoding (NoPE)? (B) How does data diversity, the number of inter-token distances seen in training, affect performance? (C) When is distance transfer learning positive or negative? We present a thorough investigation, finding that it is paramount to improve our understanding of the underlying mechanisms.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [When Passing Tests Hides Vulnerabilities: An Empirical Study of Silent Failures in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10548)
*Wenji Bai,Muhammad Waseem,Zeeshan Rasheed,Jaakko Peltonen,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究系统研究LLM代码修复代理的"静默失败"——即通过语法与功能验证但仍存在或引入安全漏洞的补丁。基于7个代理框架、GPT-4o-mini在SecurityEval和CVEfixes上的1,030条执行轨迹，经三轮定性编码确认170个静默失败，并归纳出Omission、Introduction、Inadequacy三大类及十个细粒度失败码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM自动化代码修复研究主要关注功能正确性，忽视了通过语法与功能验证却仍保留或引入安全漏洞的补丁。此类"静默失败"危害隐蔽，缺乏系统性识别与分类，故本文旨在对其进行系统化研究。

Method: 实证研究：使用7个代理框架与GPT-4o-mini，在两个安全数据集（SecurityEval、CVEfixes）上产生1,030条有效执行轨迹，通过三轮定性编码与人工验证确认静默失败。

Result: 确认170个静默失败，分三类：Omission占48.2%、Introduction占30.6%、Inadequacy占21.2%，下含10个细粒度失败码。现有测试通过式评估与LLM评审机制无法暴露或拦截这些失败；不同框架出现相似不安全方案，存在模型/提示/任务层面的共性影响，单代理与多代理系统失败特征不同。

Conclusion: 该研究为改进LLM代理式代码修复的评估提供依据，呼吁开发超越功能正确性、覆盖全部生成产物的针对性验证方法。

Abstract: LLM-based agents for automated code repair have received significant attention in recent years from both research and software engineering practice perspectives. However, limited attention has been paid to patches that pass syntactic and functional verification but still retain or introduce security vulnerabilities. The aim of this research is to systematically identify and categorize such silent failures in LLM-based agentic code repair.
  We conducted an empirical study using 1,030 valid execution traces produced by seven agent frameworks with GPT-4o-mini across two security-focused datasets, SecurityEval and CVEfixes. Through three iterations of qualitative coding and manual verification, 170 confirmed silent failures were identified. The key results are: (i) Three main categories of silent failures were identified: Omission, Introduction, and Inadequacy. Omission accounts for 48.2% of the confirmed failures, Introduction for 30.6%, and Inadequacy for 21.2%. (ii) Ten fine-grained failure codes were classified under these three categories, showing how agents omit required security controls, apply incomplete defenses, or introduce new vulnerabilities during repair. (iii) Current test-passing evaluation and LLM-based reviewer roles were insufficient to expose or intercept these failures in the confirmed cases. (iv) Similar insecure solutions appeared across different frameworks, suggesting possible shared model-, prompt-, or task-level influences, while single-agent and multi-agent systems showed different failure profiles.
  The results of this study will assist researchers and practitioners in improving the evaluation of LLM-based agentic code repair and developing targeted verification methods that go beyond functional correctness and cover all generated artifacts.

</details>


### [136] [Optimizing AI Inference Across the Deployment Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10550)
*Tejinder Singh,John Pflueger,Jeebak Mitra,Robert Lincourt,Mitchell Markow,Bhavesh A. Patel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的分析框架，用三层分类法（模型、编译器、系统策略）研究推理优化，将部署形式化为约束多目标优化问题，并给出证据协议与跨平台证据综合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI部署性能并非仅由模型架构决定，而受压缩、编译器变换与服务策略之间交互影响；已发布的基准测试常在不可比的条件下报告延迟与吞吐，难以支持部署决策。

Method: 引入三层分类法：模型级技术（量化、剪枝、蒸馏）、编译器变换（图融合、布局优化、内核自动调优）、系统策略（动态批处理、准入控制、内存分层）。将部署建模为关于精度、延迟、吞吐、内存占用与能耗的约束多目标优化问题，分析具有帕累托单调性与尺度不变性的部署排序函数；用Roofline模型刻画内存带宽层级对性能的约束，用排队模型解释负载下服务时间变化对响应时间的放大。并提出证据协议，区分实测、推导与分析性论断，仅限论文内部数值比较，并强制报告硬件、软件版本、批处理语义与热状态。

Result: 综合了三类证据：边缘平台（Jetson AGX Orin与五个推理框架）、数据中心GPU（A100、H100与三个LLM服务引擎）、以及Llama-3.1系列的量化研究。结果显示部署结果由跨层交互主导，任何单层分析都无法预测。

Conclusion: 提出约束感知的选择流程，并指出编译器-服务协同优化、跨硬件性能预测与标准化能耗报告等开放问题。

Abstract: AI deployment performance is shaped not by model architecture alone, but by interactions among compression, compiler transformations, and serving policies. Published benchmarks often report latency and throughput under incomparable conditions, limiting their use for deployment decisions. This paper presents a unified analytical treatment of inference optimization across the deployment stack. We introduce a three-layer taxonomy covering model-level techniques such as quantization, pruning, and distillation; compiler transformations such as graph fusion, layout optimization, and kernel autotuning; and system policies such as dynamic batching, admission control, and memory tiering. We formulate deployment as a constrained multi-objective optimization problem over accuracy, latency, throughput, memory footprint, and energy, and analyze a deployment-ranking functional with Pareto monotonicity and scale invariance. Roofline models show how memory-bandwidth hierarchies bound performance across precision regimes, while queuing models explain how service-time changes amplify response time under load. To improve comparability, we propose an evidence protocol that separates measured, derived, and analytical claims; limits numerical comparison to within-paper results; and requires reporting of hardware, software versions, batch semantics, and thermal state. We synthesize evidence from edge platforms, including Jetson AGX Orin and five inference frameworks; data center GPUs, including A100 and H100 with three LLM serving engines; and quantization studies across the Llama-3.1 family. The synthesis shows that deployment outcomes are governed by cross-layer interactions that no single-layer analysis can predict. We conclude with a constraint-aware selection procedure and open problems in compiler-serving co-optimization, cross-hardware performance prediction, and standardized energy reporting.

</details>


### [137] [ReqEvolve: User-Oriented Software Self-Evolution through Automatic Requirement Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10590)
*Md Asif Iqbal Fahim,Alessio Ferrari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 ReqEvolve 系统，通过自动需求工程与测试驱动开发，将高层次用户请求直接转化为可运行代码，实现用户驱动的软件自演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件自演化系统虽能自主扩展能力，但需求多来自用户且非技术化表述，需开发者先翻译为技术规格，导致功能验证延迟。

Method: ReqEvolve 整合自动需求工程与 TDD，通过澄清、规格分解、测试生成、运行时集成四个阶段，将用户请求转为可执行功能。

Result: 在 18 个项目、72 个演化案例上评估，Pass@1 达 89.2%，分别超过 SpecFix 基线 18.8%（p<0.01, r=0.79）和消融基线 32.6%（p<0.001, r=0.88）。

Conclusion: 初步证明用户驱动的自演化可行，可在开发者验证前加速需求验证。

Abstract: The paradigm of software self-evolution enables systems to autonomously extend and reconfigure their own capabilities during execution in response to technical specifications. Yet requests for new functionality often originate from end users and are rarely expressed in technical terms. As a result, developers must translate user needs into technical specifications before the system can evolve, delaying early validation of the requested functionality by preventing users from immediately observing the resulting behaviour. To address this gap, we present ReqEvolve, a runtime code generation system that enables user-driven self-evolution by accepting high-level user requests. The system integrates automatic requirements engineering (RE) and test-driven development (TDD) to transform these requests into executable functionality through clarification, specification decomposition, test generation, and runtime integration. We evaluate ReqEvolve on 72 software evolution cases across 18 projects against two baselines: SpecFix, an RE-focused code generation approach, and an ablation variant of our system. ReqEvolve achieves 89.2% Pass@1, outperforming SpecFix by 18.8% (p < 0.01, r = 0.79, large effect) and the ablation baseline by 32.6% (p < 0.001, r = 0.88, large effect). These results provide initial evidence that user-driven self-evolution is a viable paradigm for autonomously extending software capabilities from user requests, thereby accelerating requirements validation prior to developer verification.

</details>


### [138] [Generative AI for trustworthy systems - Towards a health check model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10595)
*Jan Bosch,Rick Kazman,Henry Muccini,Helena Holmström Olsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出“可信自主性健康检查模型”，用八维度结构取代单一成熟度模型来刻画组织在GenAI辅助软件工程中的信任建立方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI在软件密集系统中快速应用，但现有分析工具（尤其是单维成熟度模型）将重要的配置差异压缩为单一递进轴，无法反映组织间在信任建立方式上的多样性。

Method: 基于对电信、汽车、国防、航空、银行、能源、政府及企业软件服务领域十八位资深从业者的归纳式访谈研究，构建多维诊断模型：八维度分为系统层（代理权限范围、保障机制、数据可信性、架构隔离、可追溯性与可理解性）与组织层（治理、人类监督姿态、劳动力能力可持续性），各含五级序数尺度；并叠加运作型、工程型、统计型、隔离型四种信任范式。

Result: 模型为诊断性而非规范性工具，支持跨组织比较、揭示配置权衡、定位组织在共享空间中的位置而不强加单一演进路径；高层级并非必然更优。提出“对齐假设”：有效可信性受代理权限范围与其支撑维度匹配度的约束，错配会导致病理性风险或不必要摩擦。

Conclusion: 该模型为从业者提供了应用框架，目标是根据组织领域与所选信任范式实现跨维度对齐，未来需开展实证验证。

Abstract: The adoption of generative AI in software-intensive systems is proceeding rapidly, but current analytical instruments - principally unidimensional maturity models - compress important configurational variation into a single progressive axis. Drawing on an inductive interview study of eighteen senior practitioners across telecommunications, automotive, defence, aviation, banking, energy, government, and enterprise software services, this paper presents the Trustworthy Autonomy Health Check Model: a structured, multidimensional instrument for characterizing how organizations establish trust in GenAI-assisted software engineering. The model organizes eight empirically grounded dimensions into a system layer (Scope of Agent Authority, Assurance Mechanisms, Data Trustworthiness, Architectural Containment, Traceability & Comprehensibility) and an organizational layer (Governance, Human Oversight Posture, Workforce Capability Sustainability), each on a five-level ordinal scale. A cross-cutting overlay of four trust paradigms - operational, engineering, statistical, and containment-based - captures how trust is established, complementing the dimensions capturing what must be trustworthy. The model is diagnostic rather than prescriptive: it supports cross-organizational comparison, surfaces configurational trade-offs, and locates an organization in a shared space without imposing a single progression path. Higher levels are not inherently better; the goal is alignment across dimensions appropriate to the organization's domain and chosen trust paradigm. This is formalized as the alignment hypothesis: effective trustworthiness is constrained by the fit between the scope of agent authority and the dimensions enabling it, with misalignment producing pathological risk or unnecessary friction. We discuss how practitioners can apply the model and outline directions for empirical validation.

</details>


### [139] [On the Relation between Code Quality and Machine Learning Performance: A Large-scale Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10610)
*Marius Mignard,Steven Costiou,Anne Etien*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 大规模研究26.5万个Kaggle笔记本，发现通用Python代码质量与ML性能无关，而ML特定规则违规与性能弱负相关；流行度不反映质量或性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML社区默认代码质量与模型性能无关，并常用流行度、作者专业度作为代码质量代理，但这些假设缺乏实证检验。

Method: 对265,363个Kaggle竞赛Python笔记本进行实证分析，用Pylint评估通用代码质量，用SonarQube的34条数据科学/ML规则评估ML特定质量问题，并分析流行度与作者专业度的关系。

Result: 通用Python代码质量与ML性能相关性可忽略或不显著；ML特定违规与性能呈持续的小幅负相关。流行度不提供代码质量或性能信息；代码专业度也无信息，但竞赛经验与更好性能、更少ML违规及略多Python错误/重构违规相关。

Conclusion: 代码质量与ML性能的关系取决于质量定义；应区分通用代码质量与ML特定实践，且不应以流行度或作者专业度作为质量或性能的可靠代理。

Abstract: Context: Computational notebooks are the standard environment for machine learning (ML) development. Within the ML community, model performance is often the primary considered metric, and code quality is treated as a secondary concern. This prioritization relies on a largely untested assumption that code quality and ML performance are unrelated. Practitioners also reuse existing code that may come from notebooks selected through social signals (popularity, author expertise) whose reliability as quality proxies has never been assessed. Objective: We empirically investigated the relationship between code quality and ML performance in notebooks, and evaluated whether popularity and author expertise give indication on code quality or performance. Method: We conducted a large-scale empirical study of 265,363 Python notebooks submitted to Kaggle competitions. We assessed code quality with two static analysis tools: Pylint, capturing general Python code quality, and SonarQube, configured with a profile of 34 rules targeting data-science and ML-specific practices. Results: The relationship between code quality and performance depends on the notion of quality considered. General Python code quality is decoupled from ML performance, showing negligible or non-significant correlations across all observations. In contrast, ML-specific violations exhibit a consistent, small negative association with performance that persists across all observations. The popularity of a notebook does not give information on the code quality or performance. Code expertise provides no information on quality or performance, but competition expertise correlates with better performance, fewer ML-specific violations, and slightly more Python errors and refactoring violations.

</details>


### [140] [AI Safety: Not Optional, Not Later](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10630)
*Qinghua Lu,Yoshua Bengio*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种"安全即设计"的保障架构，将模型层监督（如Scientist AI）与系统层控制、独立验证、监控、证据基础设施及治理机制相结合，以应对多层级的AI安全失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事故表明AI安全失效往往发生在多个层面，仅靠单一层级（如模型层）的防护不足以保障安全，需要跨层级的整体性设计。

Method: 提出安全即设计的保障架构，整合模型层监督（例如Scientist AI）、系统层对脚手架与执行框架的控制、独立验证、监控与证据基础设施，并由治理机制支撑问责与证据互操作性。

Result: 摘要未提供具体实验结果或量化评估。

Conclusion: 需通过多层协同的架构化设计来实现AI安全保障，兼顾技术控制与治理问责。

Abstract: Incidents show that AI safety failures often arise across multiple layers. We present a safety-by-design assurance architecture combining model-level supervision, such as Scientist AI, with system-level controls over scaffolds and harnesses, independent verification, monitoring, and evidence infrastructure, supported by governance for accountability and evidence interoperability.

</details>


### [141] [Numbat: Building and Verifying a Self-Contained Machine-Learning Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10632)
*Thang Tran,Lan Dang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文构建并验证了 numbat——一个用单一通用语言 Zig 编写、无第三方运行时依赖的机器学习栈，并通过五级差分验证协议对齐参考实现，最终训练出与参考性能相当的 YOLOv8m 检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习系统几乎都建立在少数几个大型 Python 编排框架之上，继承了数百个版本耦合包的环境、独立的部署导出链，以及研究与产品所用语言割裂等工程成本。作者希望用一个通用语言（Zig）构建无第三方运行时依赖的完整 ML 栈来消除这些成本。同时指出验证这类栈是更困难的一半工作：有缺陷的训练很少直接失败，而是静默收敛到略差的模型。

Method: 整个栈用 Zig 编写，覆盖张量计算、自动微分、神经网络模块、混合精度、多 GPU 训练、数据加载与监控；通过 SDK 以稳定的、可增叠加版本的 C ABI（超 1400 个入口点）暴露，并提供六种语言绑定；临床领域平面把监管要求编码为可执行验收门。验证上把广泛使用的参考实现当作可执行规范，进行五级验证，从算子梯度检查到针对同机参考运行的自动化轨迹门控，形成轨迹级差分预言机。

Result: 验证协议揭示了十个静默的配方差异，并记录了其机制与症状。验收测试是在 COCO 2017 上从随机初始化训练 25.9M 参数的 YOLOv8m 类检测器，跑满 500 个 epoch：导出权重在官方协议下由参考栈自身验证器评分达到 0.4956 mAP50-95（已发布端点 0.502），单 GPU 步时在相同硬件上与参考持平。权重、逐 epoch 指标与完整运行清单均已发布。

Conclusion: numbat 证明了可以用单一通用语言、无第三方运行时依赖构建覆盖全流程的 ML 栈，并通过以参考实现为规范的差分验证达到与主流栈相当的性能；轨迹级差分预言机与可执行验收门为验证此类静默失败型系统提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Machine-learning systems are built almost exclusively on a few large Python-orchestrated frameworks, and they inherit those stacks' engineering costs: environments of hundreds of version-coupled packages, separate export toolchains for deployment, and the split between the language research is written in and the language products ship in. We report on the construction and verification of numbat, a machine-learning stack written in one general-purpose language (Zig) with no third-party runtime dependencies. The stack spans tensor computation, automatic differentiation, neural-network modules, mixed precision, multi-GPU training, data loading and monitoring; an SDK exposes it behind a stable, additively versioned C ABI of over 1,400 entry points, with bindings for six languages; and its clinical domain planes encode regulatory requirements as executable acceptance gates rather than documentation. Verifying such a stack is the harder half of building it: a defective training run rarely fails, it converges quietly to a slightly worse model. We treat a widely used reference implementation as an executable specification and verify against it at five levels, from operator gradient checks to an automated trajectory gate against a same-machine reference run - the arrangement our companion study formalizes as a trajectory-level differential oracle. The protocol surfaced ten silent recipe divergences, which we catalog with mechanisms and symptoms. As the acceptance test, we train a 25.9M-parameter detector of the YOLOv8m class from random initialization on COCO 2017 for the full 500-epoch schedule: the exported weights score 0.4956 mAP50-95 under the official protocol, scored by the reference stack's own validator (published endpoint 0.502), with single-GPU step time at parity on identical hardware. Weights, per-epoch metrics and the full run manifest are released.

</details>


### [142] [Governed Human-AI Prioritization Under Uncertainty: Adaptive Estimation and Dependency-Constrained Portfolio Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10648)
*Azzeddine Ihsine,Sara Ihsine*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究D-POAF决策实践中的五个量化算子（BVS、ERS、PVS、CCS、ODP），通过受控合成实验刻画其在已知潜变量和误差过程下的行为，为证据加权的人机协同优先级排序建立量化基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI原生软件工程中，优先级决策混合了人类判断、历史类比、参数估计和AI预测。问题不仅是排序候选工作，更是如何治理异质估计、不确定性、战略参数、依赖关系和有限容量，且保持可检查、可校准。

Method: 围绕五个量化算子（BVS、ERS、PVS、CCS、ODP），通过受控合成实验，在已知潜变量和显式误差过程下刻画其行为；并进行可靠性加权聚合、自适应可靠性加权、模型-集体分歧检测，以及800个依赖约束组合实例的测试。

Result: BVS权重中度扰动保持全局排序（Spearman中位数0.986），但更广泛战略变化使top-10%重叠降至0.788；可靠性加权工作量聚合MAE 0.616，较最佳单一估计器（1.073）降低42.6%，优于简单均值（0.638）；估计器漂移下自适应加权使RMSE降低4.5%；模型-集体分歧检测高误差估计ROC-AUC 0.906；800实例中价值-努力比平均目标比0.962、ODP距离0.953，bootstrap确认其小幅但系统性优势。

Conclusion: 为证据加权的人机优先级排序奠定量化基础，并界定了最短路径ODP建模与一般发布组合选择之间的优化边界。

Abstract: AI-native software engineering increasingly combines human judgment, historical analogy, parametric estimation, and AI-generated forecasts inside the same prioritization decision. The resulting problem is not merely how to rank candidate work, but how to govern heterogeneous estimates, uncertainty, strategic parameters, dependencies, and limited capacity in a way that remains inspectable and recalibratable. We study five quantitative operators used in the D-POAF decision practice: Business Value Score (BVS), Effort and Risk Score (ERS), Prioritization Value Score (PVS), Collective Calibration Score (CCS), and Optimal Development Path (ODP). Controlled synthetic experiments characterize their behavior under known latent variables and explicit error processes. Moderate BVS-weight perturbations preserved global rankings (median Spearman 0.986), while broader strategic changes reduced top-10% overlap to 0.788. Reliability-weighted effort aggregation achieved MAE 0.616, a 42.6% reduction relative to the best individual estimator (MAE 1.073), and outperformed a simple mean (MAE 0.638). Under estimator drift, adaptive reliability weighting reduced average RMSE by 4.5% relative to equal weighting. Model-collective divergence detected the highest-error priority estimates with ROC-AUC 0.906. In 800 dependency-constrained portfolio instances, value-to-effort achieved mean objective ratio 0.962 and ODP distance 0.953 against the exact optimum; bootstrap intervals confirm a small but systematic advantage for value-to-effort under the declared objective. These results establish a quantitative basis for evidence-weighted human-AI prioritization and define the optimization boundary between shortest-path ODP formulations and general release-portfolio selection.

</details>


### [143] [LLMVul: A Vulnerability-Labeled Dataset of LLM-Generated C/C++ Functions from Real Production Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10945)
*Mohammad Farhad,Shuvalaxmi Dass*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLMVul 是一个从真实生产仓库挖掘的、带漏洞标签的 LLM 生成 C/C++ 函数数据集，含 21,430 个函数、1,540 个漏洞函数及 17 类 CWE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞数据集多聚焦人工编写代码或受控提示环境，难以研究真实软件项目中 LLM 生成代码的安全弱点。

Method: 通过 commit 元数据与 AI 相关作者证据等溯源信号，从 GitHub 四年 AI 辅助开发活动挖掘数据；经过滤去重后，用静态分析与模式匹配的集成方法打漏洞标签并标注 CWE，另做人工标注验证（Cohen's kappa=0.79）。

Result: 数据集含 21,430 个 C/C++ 函数（来自 226 个仓库），其中 1,540 个为集成判定的漏洞函数，覆盖 17 个 CWE 类别，规模远超现有同类基准。

Conclusion: LLMVul 保留代码级漏洞标签与生成/溯源元数据，支持漏洞检测、LLM 代码安全评估及 AI 辅助开发模式分析，数据已公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate and assist with software development, yet existing vulnerability datasets largely focus on human-written code or controlled prompting environments. This limits the ability to study security weaknesses in LLM-generated code as it appears in real-world software projects. We present LLMVul, a vulnerability-labeled dataset of LLM-generated C/C++ functions mined from real production repositories. We mine AI-assisted development activity from GitHub over a 4 year period, from November 13, 2022 to September 3, 2026, using provenance signals such as commit metadata and AI-related authorship evidence. After filtering and deduplication, LLMVul contains 21,430 unique C/C++ functions from 226 repositories, together with repository, commit, function, provenance, and AI-tool metadata. We establish vulnerability labels using an ensemble of complementary static-analysis and pattern-based techniques and assign Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) categories to confirmed vulnerable functions. To assess labeling reliability, we additionally conduct independent manual annotation and measure inter-rater agreement using Cohen's kappa ($k=0.79$). LLMVul contains 1,540 ensemble-vulnerable functions spanning 17 unique CWE categories, providing substantially more real-world LLM-generated vulnerable C/C++ functions than existing vulnerability-oriented LLM code benchmarks. By preserving both code-level vulnerability labels and generation/provenance metadata, LLMVul enables reproducible research on vulnerability detection, security evaluation of LLM-generated code, and analysis of vulnerability patterns in AI-assisted software development. The LLMVul dataset is publicly available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22668216.

</details>


### [144] [What a Random Draw from the MCP Registry Contains, and What Tool-Use Benchmarks Contain Instead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10962)
*Haseeb Mohammed Afsar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对24,135个MCP服务器注册表进行无修复概率抽样，抽取400个npm/stdio服务器实际探测。仅48.8%能完成初始化握手（手工筛选集为66.7%），主要失败原因是服务器根本无法启动（37.5%），而非缺少凭证。运行成功的服务器硬性合规完美（2,766个工具零JSON Schema致命违规），但可选安全注解缺失率高达58.8%。与基准语料对比发现：真实MCP工具跨作者近重复为0.0%，BFCL v4达16.7%（多在独立任务间），UltraTool仅0.3%；而BFCL和UltraTool原始数据中分别有68.8%和85.6%为完全重复行，提示未全局去重的统计衡量的是重复而非工具本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCP服务器生态研究在抽样时存在隐性选择偏差：使用参考集、热门列表、手工筛选框架，或通过流程修复服务器使其能运行。这些做法系统性地高估了服务器的实际可用性与质量。作者希望报告未经修复的概率抽样所揭示的真实现状。

Method: 从24,135个服务器的注册表普查中，以公开随机种子抽取400个npm/stdio服务器，逐一进行网络探测（over the wire）。测量维度包括：初始化握手成功率、失败原因分类、JSON Schema合规性、可选安全注解缺失率。随后用统一方法将真实MCP工具描述与两个工具使用基准语料（BFCL v4、UltraTool）进行近重复分析（cosine 0.70阈值），并统计原始数据中的完全重复行比例。

Result: 仅48.8%完成initialize握手，手工筛选框架为66.7%；主要失败为服务器从未启动（37.5%），缺少凭证仅13.3%。运行成功的195个服务器中，2,766个工具零致命JSON Schema违规。工具级安全注解缺失率随机抽样58.8%，筛选集41.5%。真实MCP工具cosine 0.70近重复率2.8%且全部发生在单个服务器内部，跨作者为0.0%。BFCL v4近重复16.7%（其中16.4个百分点位于独立呈现的任务之间），UltraTool仅0.3%。原始BFCL行68.8%、UltraTool行85.6%为完全名称+描述重复，真实MCP仅0.4%。

Conclusion: 现有MCP生态研究因抽样偏差而高估服务器可用性与质量；真实可运行服务器的硬性协议合规性极好，但安全注解普遍缺失。BFCL的高近重复率是其自身特性而非合成语料的通病。未做全局去重的基准统计实质衡量的是重复而非工具多样性。所有数字均可通过公开脚本和随机种子复现。

Abstract: Studies of the Model Context Protocol (MCP) server ecosystem draw their samples in ways that quietly select for servers that work: reference sets, popularity lists, hand-curated frames, or pipelines that repair a server until it starts. We report what an unrepaired probability sample actually contains. From a 24,135-server registry census we draw 400 npm/stdio servers with a published seed and probe each one over the wire. Only 48.8% complete an initialize handshake, against 66.7% for a hand-curated frame measured with the same instrument, and the dominant failure is not missing credentials (13.3%) but servers that never start at all (37.5%). Among the 195 that do run, hard conformance is total: zero fatal JSON Schema violations across 2,766 advertised tools. Optional safety annotations are the real variance, and the tool-level omission rate on a random draw is 58.8% against 41.5% on the curated frame, so curation flatters this figure too. We then compare the tool descriptions these servers advertise against two tool-use benchmark corpora using one method held constant. Real MCP tools show 2.8% near-duplication at cosine 0.70, and all of it lies within single servers: cross-author near-duplication is 0.0% at every threshold tested. BFCL v4 shows 16.7%, of which 16.4 points lie between independently presented tasks. UltraTool shows 0.3%, cleaner than real tools, so this is a property of BFCL and not of synthetic corpora as a class. Separately, 68.8% of raw BFCL rows and 85.6% of raw UltraTool rows are exact name-plus-description repeats, against 0.4% for real MCP, so any statistic computed over these releases without global deduplication measures repetition rather than tools. All figures regenerate from released scripts and a published seed.

</details>


### [145] [Engineering Reliable Commit Gates for Agentic AI: Cost-Aware Verification Portfolios under Common-Mode Data Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10969)
*Zihao Zheng,Baichuan Li,Junyi Yao,Jiayu Long*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出VP-CONTROL——一种带成本感知提交门（commit gate）的运行时保障设计与确定性基准，研究验证器故障继承问题，强调证据来源独立性优于模型多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统会执行改变状态的动作，但额外验证器可能继承同一上游故障，导致验证失效，因此需要更可靠的提交门设计。

Method: 构建48个任务模板，覆盖6类故障机制，共2880个场景；用固定调用2x2实验区分验证器模型多样性与证据来源多样性；提出仅用部署可观测元数据的组合控制器选择验证计划；并进行预注册的实时HTTP/SQLite研究，测试并发写入与响应丢失。

Result: 共享证据下跨模型投票仍批准62.9%不安全提案，而独立来源仅22.9%，来源效应达40.9个百分点，模型多样性为11.3；组合控制器在名义5%目标下实现1.9%不安全执行和38.2%安全自动化覆盖；匹配预算组合优于固定策略；但迁移有限，未见故障族风险16-26%，FinQA小验证器未复现来源效应；实时研究中，事务部分防护仅覆盖已知故障，完整原子防护216次无安全副作用，幂等请求标识避免重复效应。

Conclusion: 结果支持显式证据谱系、成本感知选择与提交时强制执行，同时揭示近似校准和本地工具泛化的局限。

Abstract: Agentic systems commit state-changing actions, but additional verifiers can inherit the same upstream fault. We present VP-CONTROL, a runtime-assurance design and deterministic benchmark for cost-aware commit gates. Its 48 task templates yield 2,880 scenarios across six fault regimes. A fixed-call 2 x 2 experiment separates verifier-model diversity from evidence-source diversity. On frozen proposals from two local actor families, a cross-model vote over shared evidence approves 62.9% of unsafe proposals, versus 22.9% with an independent source. The source effect is 40.9 percentage points, compared with 11.3 for model diversity. A portfolio controller selects verification plans using only deployment-observable metadata. Approximate cluster-adjusted calibration at a nominal 5% per-task target yields 1.9% unsafe execution and 38.2% automated safe coverage on the locked test. Matched-budget portfolios also improve on fixed verification policies. Transfer remains conditional: unseen fault families yield 16-26% risk, and a FinQA check fails to reproduce the source effect with the tested small verifiers. A preregistered live HTTP/SQLite study tests concurrent writes and lost responses. After-check races defeat verifier-only gates; transactional partial guards prevent only covered failures, while a full atomic guard records no unsafe effects across 216 episodes. Idempotent request identifiers prevent duplicate effects after lost responses. The results motivate explicit evidence lineage, cost-aware selection, and commit-time enforcement, while exposing the limits of approximate calibration and local-tool generalization.

</details>


### [146] [RCL: A Retrieval-Confidence Layer for Detecting Insufficient Context in Enterprise Retrieval-Augmented Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11023)
*Chandra Mohan Ravuri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RCL（Retrieval-Confidence Layer），一个插入检索与生成之间的轻量模块，通过结合调用图衍生的结构覆盖分数与新颖性分数，在生成前检测私有代码库中检索是否充分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG代码生成研究多基于公开仓库，模型参数化知识可弥补检索不足；但在企业私有代码库中，私有API、内部框架和团队约定完全处于预训练分布之外，导致即使用完美检索仍在下游API使用出错。现有研究既未解决"检索本身对私有代码查询是否结构充分"的问题，也未从模型内部置信度评估检索充分性。

Method: RCL模块包含两个评分函数：(1)调用图衍生的结构覆盖分数；(2)估计查询对模型先验之外知识依赖度的新颖性分数。当置信度低于校准阈值时，触发针对性补充检索或将输出标记为人工审查，而非在上下文不完整时静默生成。采用将合成内部API注入开源Java仓库构建的私有代码基准进行评测。

Result: 报告了RCL与仅基于相似度的检索在生成正确性上的对比结果（见第7节）。

Conclusion: 本文立场是：从结构而非模型内部置信度评估检索充分性，是构建私有企业环境下更安全代码生成系统的一个独特且当前未被充分解决的信号。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) for code generation has been studied extensively on public repositories, where a model's parametric knowledge often compensates for imperfect retrieval. This breaks down in enterprise codebases, where private APIs, internal frameworks, and undocumented team conventions fall entirely outside any model's pretraining distribution. Recent work on private-library code generation shows that even oracle (perfect) retrieval does not eliminate errors, but locates failures downstream in API usage; separately, confidence-gated retrieval has been studied for open-domain question answering using model-internal confidence. Neither addresses whether retrieval itself was structurally sufficient for a private-code query before generation begins. We introduce RCL (Retrieval-Confidence Layer), a lightweight module inserted between retrieval and generation that combines a call-graph-derived structural coverage score with a novelty score estimating a query's dependence on knowledge outside the model's prior, to detect insufficient retrieval before generation occurs. When confidence falls below a calibrated threshold, RCL triggers a targeted follow-up retrieval or labels the output for human review, rather than generating silently against incomplete context. We describe RCL's architecture, formalize its scoring functions, and propose an evaluation methodology using a private-code benchmark built by injecting synthetic internal APIs into open-source Java repositories, simulating the enterprise condition without proprietary code. We report results (Section 7) comparing RCL against similarity-only retrieval on generation correctness. Our position is that retrieval sufficiency, assessed structurally rather than from model-internal confidence, is a distinct and currently underaddressed signal for building safer code-generation systems in private, enterprise settings.

</details>


### [147] [SaltBench: A Referee-Gated Protocol for Measuring Method Effects in Machine-Checked Software Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11076)
*Jason Hickey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SaltBench 是一个基准协议，用于测量机器裁判（证明内核、程序验证器或隐藏测试套件）如何改变编码智能体的工作方式，通过外部决定、隔离探针、日期冻结和预算停止等设计确保结果不可事后叙述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是回答"机器裁判如何改变编码智能体的工作方式"，并建立一个能让裁判效应可测量、且答案无法在事后被主观叙述篡改的基准协议。

Method: 以"座位"（标准harness中的智能体会话）为基准对象，测试五个 Rust 组件（固定 Verus 工具链），以隐藏测试套件为裁判。智能体被隔离网络、参考解和harness，隔离性通过探针在评分前验证；每次运行需日期冻结并登记预测；预算停止即终止。比较四组：普通智能体；被指示编写规格并验证代码的智能体（简化方法）；以及两组先验提供规格、经日期修订扩展至 k=4 的智能体。

Result: 被指示编写规格并验证的组在全部五个组件上成本更高，且具有实际差距：在任何解读下溢价均未超过 2.8879×，三个最便宜组件低于 1.4×。溢价在最小组件上接近 1，随规模增大而上升。注册的符号检验未得出结论（4 中 3，p = 0.3125，所有溢价低于可分辨下限）。

Conclusion: 该成本溢价上限是该特定总体的性质，不能推广到更大的总体。作者公开了完整记录。

Abstract: SaltBench is a benchmark protocol for one question: How does a machine referee change the way a coding agent works? A machine referee --- a proof kernel, a program verifier, or a withheld test suite --- decides what an agent's work is worth, and the agent cannot argue with it. Here we report a protocol that makes the referee's effect measurable and whose answers cannot be narrated afterwards: every outcome is decided outside the agent's own toolchain; the agent is walled off from the network, the reference solutions and the harness itself, and the wall is tested by probes that try to breach it before any scored run, so the isolation is observed rather than assumed; every run is authorized by a dated freeze with its predictions registered; and a budget stop is a halt, never a failure. In this study, the subject of the benchmark is a ``seat'', meaning an agent session in its standard harness. We tested five systems components, all authored in Rust under a pinned Verus toolchain, with a withheld test suite as the referee for each. Four arms are tested: a plain agent; an agent that is also instructed to create a specification and verify the code against it, in a reduced rendering of the method, as registered; and two arms where the specification is provided a priori, extended under a dated amendment to $k=4$, where the registered sign test reached no verdict (3 of 4, $p = 0.3125$, every premium below the resolvable floor). We found that the arm instructed to specify and verify cost more on all five components, and by a practical margin: across these five components no premium exceeded $2.8879\times$ under either reading of the declared set, and the three cheapest sat below $1.4\times$. That bound is a property of this population and not a promise about larger ones: the premium runs near $1$ on the smallest components and rises with size. We publish the complete record.

</details>


### [148] [A Model-Centric DevOps Architecture for DEVS-Based Digital Twin Simulation Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11122)
*Arnis Lektauers,Gusts Linkevičs,Guntis Mosāns,Arina Fokina,Rasa Gulbe*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向DEVS数字孪生仿真的模型中心DevOps架构，将模型作为一等交付物，通过声明式YAML定义、CI/CD验证与Kubernetes微服务实现全生命周期管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DEVS引擎具备严谨形式化基础，但缺乏版本管理、自动验证与云原生持续交付支持，导致模型生命周期管理多为临时性方案。

Method: 将仿真模型建模为DevOps一等制品，用声明式YAML定义并形式化映射到multiPDEVS；在CI/CD流水线中做结构与语义验证，产出不可变的版本化制品；平台以Kubernetes容器化微服务形式部署，并改造引擎以支持状态外化与生命周期控制。

Result: 以里加22路公交走廊为案例，完整验证了生命周期流程，并报告了含约47,870个DEVS原子组件场景下的单容器引擎吞吐量。

Conclusion: 该架构为DEVS数字孪生提供可版本化、可验证、云原生可交付的模型管理中心方案；流水线捕获统计与集群级多场景并发执行将另文研究。
