This paper presents a lightweight LiDAR-only perception pipeline tailored for CPU execution, combining ground removal, IMU-based motion compensation, DBSCAN clustering, and geometric feature-based Random Forest classification. Feature importance analysis reduced the model input from 12 to 7 features while preserving performance. Evaluated on 2,371 labeled clusters collected from real FSD events, the pipeline achieves an F1-score of 98.33% and an end-to-end runtime of 3.13 ms on CPU-only hardware. The released dataset, labeling tool, and trained models provide a practical and reproducible baseline for other resource-constrained autonomous racing teams.

</details>


### [221] [Prompt Revision as a Source of Cultural Bias in Text-to-Image Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11532)
*Aleksandra Urman,Elsa Lichtenegger,Salima Jaoua,Azza Bouleimen,Robin Forsberg,Corinna Hertweck,Stefania Ionescu,Nicolò Pagan,Ancsa Hannak,Joachim Baumann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出WORLDVIEW多语言基准（8,960提示词，15种语言，31个语言-语境配对），审计DALL-E-3、Imagen-4、GPT-Image-1.5的提示词修订层，发现该层是文化刻板印象的因果来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用文生图系统会悄然修改用户提示词，用户无法禁用或查看。而现有文化偏见审计只看最终图像、将生成视为单一流程，无法定位偏见来源。

Method: 构建WORLDVIEW多语言基准，通过三步分析审计三个系统的修订层：对各文化语境的标记程度、是否将语境压缩为狭窄词汇、该词汇是否刻板化。并在无修订层模型上对比原始与修订提示词的生成图像。

Result: 相对无语境英文基线，美国被标记最少，非西方及非英语语境被标记更重、被压缩为跨主题通用的狭窄词汇，并简化为刻板印象。对比实验确认修订层本身是此前未被记录的文化刻板化因果来源。

Conclusion: 要定位并修复文化偏见，必须审计实际部署的系统而非仅审计模型本身。

Abstract: Commercial text-to-image systems silently revise user prompts before generating images, a step users typically cannot disable or even see. Yet, existing audits of cultural bias examine only the final images and treat generation as a single pipeline, so they cannot tell where the bias originates. We introduce WORLDVIEW, a multilingual benchmark of 8,960 prompts across 15 languages and 31 language-context pairings. Using it, we audit the revision layer in three systems (DALL-E-3, Imagen-4, GPT-Image-1.5) through a three-step analysis of how heavily it marks each cultural context, whether it flattens that context into a narrow vocabulary, and whether that vocabulary is stereotypical. Relative to a no-context English baseline, the US is the least-marked context, while non-Western and non-Anglophone contexts are marked far more heavily, flattened into narrow vocabularies applied across topically diverse prompts, and reduced to recognizable cultural stereotypes. Comparing images from original versus revised prompts on models without a revision layer, we identify the layer itself as a previously undocumented, causal source of this stereotyping. To locate cultural bias, and fix it, we must audit the system as deployed, not the model alone.

</details>


### [222] [Characterizing Job Power Elasticity for Power-Flexible AI Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11542)
*Philip Colangelo,Charles Dawson,Shayan Sengupta,Ayse Coskun,Varun Sivaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统刻画LLM训练的"功率弹性"，提出功率灵活性指数(PFI)，并证明感知PFI的功率分配可在功耗约束下最大化吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM训练是数据中心电力需求增长最快的来源，电力可用性成为AI基础设施扩张瓶颈。若能让训练功耗变得灵活，可释放额外电力、抑制电价上涨并提升电网利用率，但前提是理解GPU功率降低时训练性能如何变化。

Method: 提出功率灵活性指数(PFI)——量化功率下降性能代价的归一化指标，作为SLA感知的功率控制原语；在H200上采集131次训练运行(另24次H200验证、34次H100配对)，涵盖稠密与MoE模型、预训练与微调、最多32 GPU；识别可运行时预测PFI的遥测信号；实现感知PFI的功率分配。

Result: LLM训练任务表现出显著但可变的功率弹性；遥测信号可预测PFI；感知PFI的分配在功耗约束下最大化总tokens/s。30%功率削减下，每任务恢复约1.5k tokens/s，弥合等权分配与完美信息oracle之间性能差距的63%。

Conclusion: 确立功率弹性为训练任务的可测量属性，为功率感知、电网响应的AI基础设施奠定基础。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) training is among the fastest-growing sources of electricity demand in modern data centers, and power availability is a primary bottleneck to continued AI infrastructure growth. Making the power consumption of these workloads flexible could unlock additional power for AI growth, limit increases in electricity prices, and improve the utilization of existing grid infrastructure. However, to realize this flexibility, we must first understand how the performance of training workloads changes when GPU power is reduced.
  This paper presents the first systematic characterization of \emph{job power elasticity} (the sensitivity of throughput to power reductions) in LLM training. To quantify elasticity, we introduce the \emph{Power Flexibility Index (PFI)}, a normalized metric that quantifies the performance cost of power reductions and provides a control primitive for SLA-aware power flexibility.
  We collect data from 131 LLM training runs on H200 (plus 24 H200 validation runs and 34 matched H100 runs), including both dense and mixture-of-experts models, pretraining and fine-tuning tasks, and up to 32 GPUs. We find that LLM training jobs exhibit substantial but variable power elasticity, and we identify telemetry signals that predict PFI at runtime. Finally, we demonstrate that PFI-aware power allocation maximizes total tokens/second throughput under power constraints. Under a 30\% power reduction, PFI-aware power allocation recovers ~1.5k tokens/s per job, 63\% of the performance gap between an equal-weight allocation and an oracle with perfect information. Our results establish power elasticity as a measurable property of training jobs and provide a foundation for power-aware, grid-responsive AI infrastructure.

</details>


### [223] [Enabling Knowledge Graph Understanding at Scale with the EXplore Your Graphs ENgine (EXYGEN)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11569)
*Harshdeep Singh,Yurui Zhu,Giovanni Colavizza,Matteo Romanello*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EXYGEN框架通过RAG结合结构化元数据实现KG对话式访问，无需微调即可在SciQA上取得0.419执行结果精确匹配，并提出高效并行图采样策略显著降低元数据生成成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决知识图谱对话式访问中的两个问题：LLM在仅依赖自动派生结构元数据和小样本时的text-to-SPARQL生成效果如何；以及如何从超大规模KG中高效生成所需结构元数据（元数据生成在计算上不可行）。

Method: 将VoID描述和ShEx模式整合进RAG流水线，并在SciQA上消融KG派生上下文；提出谓词覆盖感知的并行图采样策略，在保持结构多样性的同时保证计算可行性。

Result: 最佳配置（ShEx模式+检索三元组+示例问答对）达到0.419执行结果精确匹配，无需微调；发现F1等词汇指标难以预测查询正确性，且在上下文充足时大型通用LLM可超越小型代码专用模型；采样策略在OpenCitations Meta和GESIS上保持高谓词覆盖且运行时间降低80倍以上，在ORKG上是获得完整元数据的唯一可行路径。

Conclusion: 结构化模式上下文和轻量提示可大幅减少对微调的依赖，实现可扩展的KG对话式访问；但要缩小与全微调方法的差距，需减少对人工问答示例的依赖（如合成生成或执行反馈驱动方法），并在更多基准上验证。

Abstract: We present EXYGEN (EXplore Your Graphs ENgine), a framework for knowledge graph (KG) understanding that enables conversational access to KGs at scale. We address two questions in sequence. First, how effectively can LLMs perform text-to-SPARQL generation given only automatically derived structured metadata and small graph samples, rather than task-specific fine-tuning? We integrate VoID descriptions and ShEx schemas into a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline and ablate KG-derived context on the SciQA benchmark. Our best configuration -- combining ShEx schemas, retrieved triples, and example question-query pairs -- reaches an exact match of 0.419 on execution results without any LLM fine-tuning. We further find that lexical metrics such as F1 poorly predict query correctness, and that larger general-purpose LLMs can outperform smaller code-specialized ones once given sufficient context. Second, we ask how to generate the structured metadata that this method relies on from very large KGs, where KG metadata generation becomes computationally intractable. We introduce a predicate-coverage-aware parallel graph sampling strategy that preserves structural diversity while remaining computationally tractable. On OpenCitations Meta and GESIS, it retains high predicate coverage with minimal triple loss and reduces runtime by over 80x; on ORKG, sampling is not just faster but the only tractable path to obtain complete metadata. Together, these results show that structured schema context and lightweight prompting can substantially reduce reliance on fine-tuning for scalable conversational access to KGs, though closing the remaining gap to fully fine-tuned approaches will likely require reducing dependence on curated question-query exemplars -- whether through synthetic generation or an execution-feedback-driven approach -- and validating these findings beyond a single benchmark.

</details>


### [224] [Making Alternative Data Work: Context-Augmented LLMs for Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11607)
*Jihoon Kwon,Lawrence Liu,Daekyung Park,Sumin Kim,Haverty Jack,Hoyoung Lee,Katherine Bjorkman,Josh McKenney,Peter Laurelli,Nicole Kagan,Zach Golkhou,Thorsten Neumann,Edward Tong,Pete Petersen,Yoon Kim,Alejandro Lopez-Lira,Yongjae Lee,Chanyeol Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个双智能体框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）通过上下文学习，将替代数据与其他财务信息整合以预测企业未来营收表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 替代数据（如消费交易、网络流量、预测市场）能提供及时信号并补充传统公开信息，但企业级替代数据历史覆盖有限、仅与特定预测目标或企业子集相关、且分散于众多异质渠道，难以灵活融入传统预测方法。LLM具备无需任务特定参数更新即可整合异质信息的能力，因此值得探索其整合替代数据进行财务预测的潜力。

Method: 提出双智能体（two-agent）框架：第一个智能体识别每个替代数据渠道对哪些企业可能具有信息价值；第二个智能体结合企业及渠道特定的上下文，利用LLM的上下文学习预测营收。在四个商业替代数据渠道上评估。

Result: 实验表明，将替代数据与其他财务信息一同纳入上下文，相比仅使用单一来源能提升LLM的预测表现，且预测精度优于标准预测基线。

Conclusion: LLM为整合替代数据与异质财务信息提供了一种灵活且实用的方法，能有效提升企业未来财务表现的预测。

Abstract: When forecasting a firm's future financial performance, alternative data - data collected from non-traditional sources such as consumer transactions, web traffic, and prediction markets - can provide timely signals about firms' operating activities and broader market conditions. These signals may reveal information that is not captured by traditional public sources and can therefore provide complementary information for forecasting firms' future financial performance. However, firm-level alternative data often have limited historical coverage, are relevant only to specific prediction targets or subsets of firms, and are distributed across numerous heterogeneous channels, making them difficult to incorporate flexibly into conventional forecasting approaches. Meanwhile, large language models (LLMs) can interpret instructions, learn from in-context examples, and generate predictions by combining heterogeneous information without task-specific parameter updates. Motivated by this potential flexibility, we investigate whether an LLM can forecast firm performance by integrating alternative data with other financial information through in-context learning. We propose a two-agent framework that first identifies the firms for which each alternative data channel is likely to be informative and then predicts revenue using firm- and channel-specific context. We evaluate the framework across four commercial alternative data channels. In our experiments, adding alternative data in context alongside other financial information improves the LLM's forecasting relative to either source alone, and these forecasts are more accurate than those of standard forecasting baselines. These findings suggest that LLMs provide a flexible and practical approach to integrating alternative data with heterogeneous financial information.

</details>


### [225] [Distributed Optimization of Modular Production Systems using Model-based Reinforcement Learning with Inverse Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11615)
*Andreas Schwung,Steve Yuwono,Sofiene Lassoued,Dorothea Schwung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种模型强化学习框架，将近似逆过程模型引入策略训练，解耦驱动动力学与状态空间动力学，实现仅在任务空间进行RL训练，提升高度柔性模块化制造系统的自学习控制效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高度柔性、模块化的制造系统需要数据驱动的自学习控制，但直接在状态空间中进行强化学习训练效率低下，驱动动力学与状态动力学的耦合增加了学习难度。

Method: 提出一种新的基于模型的强化学习框架，在强化策略训练中引入近似逆过程模型，解耦驱动动力学与状态空间动力学；设计轻量级前馈近似逆模型架构，并将其集成到标准RL算法的策略网络中。

Result: 在包含异构生产模块的实验室模块化生产测试平台上验证，结果表明该方法在性能和训练速度方面均提升了模块化制造单元的效率，尤其对off-policy算法效果显著。

Conclusion: 通过近似逆过程模型解耦动力学，实现任务空间内的RL训练，可有效提升模块化制造系统的学习控制效率，且兼容标准RL算法。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach for data-driven self-learning control of highly flexible, modular manufacturing systems. Specifically, we employ a novel framework for model-based reinforcement learning which introduces approximate inverse process models within the training of reinforcement policies. This approach disentangles the learning of actuation dynamics and the dynamics in state space, resulting in RL-based training solely within the task space. We propose a lightweight feedforward architecture for approximate inverse models and integrate them within the policy network of standard RL algorithms. We apply the approach to a laboratory modular production testbed with heterogeneous production modules. The results underline the efficiency improvements for modular manufacturing units in terms of both performance and training speed, particularly for off-policy algorithms.

</details>


### [226] [MAPLE: Memory-Augmented Planning with Language and Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11636)
*Kesheng Chen,Yamin Hu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAPLE是一个记忆增强的规划智能体，通过自然语言请求维护优化问题，结合语言建模、数学规划与进化搜索，在NLDO基准上实现高质量动态优化更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实运营动态变化，需求、资源和优先级不断调整，需要更新数据、约束和目标。现有以孤立请求为中心的方法难以支持快速适应，无法保留既有决策或复用有用搜索结果。

Method: MAPLE结合基于语言的问题构建与数学规划、进化搜索，并保留优化程序、已接受方案、早期更新和候选解以供后续请求使用。同时提出NLDO基准，包含15条轨迹、180次更新，覆盖选择、调度、排班、路由和云资源放置。

Result: MAPLE完成全部轨迹，在线标量质量达0.951，Pareto超体积比为0.875。受控对比表明，维护可执行状态能提升更新有效性，并在大幅修改中保留有用搜索信息。

Conclusion: 通过记忆增强与可执行状态维护，MAPLE有效支持连续自然语言驱动的优化问题演化，提升动态场景下的适应性与决策一致性。

Abstract: Domain practitioners understand their business constraints but may lack operations-research expertise or dedicated support. LLM-based optimization agents translate natural-language requirements into models or solver programs that established optimization tools can execute. This progress makes optimization more accessible, but real-world operations are dynamic: changing demand, resources, and priorities require updates to data, constraints, and objectives. Methods centered on isolated requests offer limited support for rapid adaptation that preserves earlier decisions and reuses useful search results. We introduce MAPLE (Memory-Augmented Planning with Language and Evolution), an agent for maintaining optimization problems through successive natural-language requests. MAPLE combines language-based problem construction with mathematical programming and evolutionary search. It retains the optimization program, accepted plans, earlier updates, and candidate solutions for subsequent requests. We introduce NLDO, a benchmark of 15 trajectories and 180 updates spanning selection, scheduling, rostering, routing, and cloud-resource placement. In the main evaluation, MAPLE completes all trajectories and achieves online scalar quality of 0.951 and a Pareto hypervolume ratio of 0.875. Controlled comparisons further show that maintaining executable state improves update validity and can preserve useful search information across substantial revisions.

</details>


### [227] [Autonomy, Social Norms, and Alignment: Towards a Developmental Framework for Autonomous Artificial Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11660)
*Marica Notte,Ludovica Marinucci,Vieri Giuliano Santucci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文主张将AI对齐视为一个渐进的"教育"过程，通过监管沙盒等教学环境，让具身智能体在互动与协作中从简单情境出发逐步习得规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型虽强，但在动态或未知情境中依赖预存数据集与人类反馈策略不足；具身智能体需通过直接交互获取知识，而引入内在动机虽增强自主性，却使对齐人类目标更加困难，尤其在非结构化动态环境中预定义规则失效。

Method: 提出以认识论过程将规范根植于经验：从简单的、情境化的原则出发，经由经验、自主学习和与其他道德主体的合作，逐步构建复杂规则；借鉴Dennett观点，道德主体地位非天生而是依据负责任地管理递增自由度的能力逐步赋予；将监管沙盒视为AI的教学环境。

Result: 该文为概念性/理论性论述，未提供实证结果，而是论证了以发展性、教育式路径实现对齐的可行性与必要性。

Conclusion: AI对齐应作为形成性过程在动态、递增复杂度的场景中通过交互与协作逐步塑造自主行为，如同儿童学习社会规范，监管沙盒可作为实现此目标的 pedagogical 环境。

Abstract: In recent years, artificial intelligence has made extraordinary progress thanks to large-scale models capable of generalization and the generation of complex outputs. However, transferring this potential into embodied agents reveals a significant limitation: the most advanced systems rely on pre-existing datasets and human feedback strategies that are powerful but insufficient in dynamic or unknown contexts. To adapt, an agent must acquire knowledge through direct interaction with its environment. One strategy to address this challenge involves introducing higher-level mechanisms, such as intrinsic motivations, which leverage curiosity and competence, to guide exploration and learning in complex environments. While this flexibility expands autonomy, it complicates the task of ensuring agents remain aligned with human goals. Alignment, already a challenge for artificial systems in general, becomes even more complex in unstructured and dynamic contexts where predefined rules prove insufficient. To be effective and adaptable, norms must be rooted in experience through an epistemological process that starting from simple, situated principles allows for the gradual construction of more complex rules through experience, autonomous learning, and cooperation with other moral agents. Similarly to children learning social norms by exploring their environment and participating in collective practices, artificial agents must also be educated toward alignment. Following Dennett, the status of a moral agent is not innate but is attributed gradually based on the ability to responsibly manage increasing degrees of freedom. From this perspective, the regulatory sandboxes can be viewed as pedagogical environments for AI: dynamic spaces where alignment develops as a formative process, progressively shaping autonomous behaviors through interaction and cooperation in scenarios of increasing complexity.

</details>


### [228] [Geospatial AI, Dataverse Metadata, and the Study of Place-Based Government](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11674)
*Danny EBanks,Devika Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文从Harvard Dataverse构建知识图谱，组织10万余数据集为21万节点网络，分析地理元数据覆盖与政策相关性，指出地点消歧是核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Harvard Dataverse托管超15万数据集，但其地理信息由提交者以自由文本录入，从未被整合为可检索结构，导致地理元数据难以有效利用。

Method: 利用仓库公开数据与元数据构建知识图谱，将102,650个数据集组织进215,985节点、528,003条边的网络，连接数据集与关键词、出版物、主题、期刊和地点；并进行关键词检索及多域案例分析与扩展用例。

Result: 43,991个数据集（42.9%）带有至少一个地理字段，96.9%节点位于单一连通分量；保守关键词检索识别出7,654个（17.4%）地理标记数据集与政策直接相关，选举与立法为最大集群，其次为政府行政、卫生政策、交通和教育；五个数据集展示跨政策域与空间尺度行为，扩展用例展示社区语言模型、立场检测与党派语言桥接如何将话语关联到地点。

Conclusion: 核心障碍是地点消歧：同一地点呈现为多个不连通节点；该图谱为AI驱动的元数据丰富与实体消歧提供具体场景，同时记录了数据覆盖偏向美国城市级数据的偏差。

Abstract: Harvard Dataverse hosts over 150,000 research datasets, but the geographic information those datasets carry is entered as free text by depositors and has never been assembled into a searchable structure. We construct a knowledge graph from the repository's public data and metadata, organizing 102,650 datasets within a 215,985-node network of 528,003 edges linking datasets to keywords, publications, subjects, journals, and locations. Of those datasets, 43,991 (42.9 percent) carry at least one geospatial field, geographic coverage, geographic unit, or a bounding box and 96.9 percent of all nodes sit in a single connected component, so datasets remain reachable from one another even when their geospatial metadata share nothing in common. A conservative keyword search identifies 7,654 geospatially tagged datasets (17.4 percent) as directly policy-relevant, with elections and legislatures the largest cluster, followed by government administration, health policy, transportation, and education. Five datasets illustrate how this metadata behaves across policy domains and spatial scales, and an extended use case shows how community language models, stance detection with geographic aggregation, and partisan language bridging tools can attach discourse to place. The central obstacle is place resolution: the same location appears as many disconnected nodes. We argue that the graph provides a concrete setting for developing AI-driven metadata enrichment and entity resolution, and we document its coverage skew toward American, city-level data.

</details>


### [229] [COBRA-Skills: Contextual Bandit-Guided Evolution for Agent Skill Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11682)
*Pingchen Lu,Xiangyi Wang,Xiang Li,Jie Mao,Zikun Qu,Junfeng Luo,Yao Shu,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Zhongxiang Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COBRA-Skills 将技能优化建模为预算受限的序列优化，结合 contextual bandit 优先排序与证据驱动的技能进化，在六个基准、三个模型上取得最佳平均性能，成本较 SkillOpt 降低 55-58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能优化方法依赖昂贵的基于执行的评估和大量任务数据，效率低。

Method: 将技能优化形式化为动态候选空间上的预算序列优化；用 contextual bandit 引导的优先排序，将评估选择性分配给有潜力或信息量大的候选；通过执行反馈持续进化技能种群。

Result: 在六个异构 agent 基准和三个目标模型上，平均性能最强；相较 SkillOpt 降低优化成本 55-58%；每个基准仅用 50 个优化样本；对 agent harness 变化鲁棒，且目标模型自身用于技能生成与精炼时仍有效。

Conclusion: COBRA-Skills 是一种高效、鲁棒的技能优化框架，显著降低评估成本与数据需求。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can benefit from reusable skills distilled from prior task experience, yet existing skill optimization methods often rely on costly execution-based evaluation and substantial task data. We introduce \textbf{COBRA-Skills}, an efficient framework that formulates skill optimization as budgeted sequential optimization over a dynamically evolving candidate space. COBRA-Skills couples contextual-bandit-guided prioritization with evidence-grounded skill evolution, selectively allocating evaluations to promising or informative candidates while continually refining the skill population from execution feedback. Across six heterogeneous agent benchmarks and three target models, COBRA-Skills consistently achieves the strongest average performance among compared methods, while reducing optimization cost by 55--58\% relative to SkillOpt and using only 50 unique optimization examples per benchmark. Further analyses show that COBRA-Skills remains robust to changes in the agent harness and performs effectively when the target model itself is used for skill generation and refinement.

</details>


### [230] [When Agents Disagree: Bayesian Backward Reasoning as a Label-Free Anchor for Multi-Agent Collective Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11709)
*Ken Chen,Wei Wang,Sachith Seneviratne,Hansani Weeratunge,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯反向推理构建反向后验，用Jensen-Shannon散度按跨路径一致性排序智能体，设计MinJS、FwdJS和LogLin三种聚合策略，在DDXPlus多LLM上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多LLM智能体答案冲突时，现有投票、选举规则和LLM裁判等方法均基于前向推理（evidence→label单向映射），其聚合的估计共享同一因子分解方式，易继承前向池内的相关错误。作者希望引入不同因子分解路径以减少误差共享。

Method: 通过显式似然对每个实例进行贝叶斯反向推理，构建反向后验，与前向后验形成不同因子分解的近似。用Jensen-Shannon散度衡量跨路径一致性以对智能体排序，并据此提出三种策略：硬选择（MinJS）、软重加权（FwdJS）和对数线性融合（LogLin）；有标注数据时再用轻量两阶段校准优化反向锚点。

Result: 在DDXPlus上对五个LLM骨干评估，所提策略均有稳定提升：MinJS在所有骨干上优于随机选择，FwdJS总体优于最强基线，LogLin在所有方法中表现最佳，且智能体分歧子集上增益最大。反向后验单独准确率虽较弱，但作为锚点比纯前向方案更有用。

Conclusion: 反向后验提供与前向互补的信息，跨路径一致性是有效的聚合信号；结合校准可进一步提升集体决策性能。

Abstract: When multiple LLM agents yield conflicting answers, the decision-making process dictates whether agent diversity improves performance or merely compounds shared errors. Existing collective decision-making methods, including voting, electoral rules, and LLM judges, rely on forward reasoning: they map evidence to labels in one direction. Although these methods can combine diverse forward traces, they still aggregate estimates that share this evidence-to-label factorization and can inherit correlated errors within the forward pool. We therefore construct a reverse posterior for each instance through Bayesian backward reasoning from an explicit likelihood. The forward and reverse posteriors provide differently factorized approximations of the underlying posterior. Because estimates from different factorizations may tend to share the same error less often, we use Jensen-Shannon divergence to rank agents by cross-path consistency. This cross-path consistency signal underlies three strategies: hard selection (MinJS), soft reweighting (FwdJS), and log-linear fusion (LogLin). Evaluated on DDXPlus across five LLM backbones, our proposed strategies show consistent improvements: MinJS outperforms random selection across all backbones, FwdJS generally improves over the strongest baseline, and LogLin achieves the best performance among the evaluated methods, with its largest gains on the subset where the agents disagree. Despite its weaker standalone accuracy, the reverse posterior serves as a more useful anchor than forward-only alternatives, providing complementary information for collective decision-making. When labeled data are available, a lightweight two-stage calibration can further refine the reverse anchor and improve aggregation performance.

</details>


### [231] [SIRF: A Spec-Internalized Risk Foundation Model for Industrial Content Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11752)
*Suwan Wu,Yumeng Lin,Pengcheng Yuan,Xiaolong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SIRF将平台复杂风控策略通过EntiGraph、MAGA改写和账号级CoT合成，经持续预训练内化进模型权重，实现高精度、超低延迟的仅输出判定部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业内容风控的真实部署约束不是平均精度，而是在高精度与秒级延迟下能自动处理多少风险。

Method: 提出SIRF（规范内化风险基础模型）：无需额外人工标注，通过EntiGraph、MAGA改写与账号级链式思维（CoT）合成策略数据，再用持续预训练（CPT）将规则内化至权重。采用同源对照（Qwen3-8B-SFT vs. SIRF-8B-SFT，策略注入与仅判定输出形式相同，仅差策略驱动的CPT）归因收益。

Result: SIRF-8B-SFT达到71.3% Black Recall@P95，较基线提升15.1个百分点，仅用约70M CPT token且不损害通用能力；在同接口下可匹配或超越更大模型。部署为树模型裁决层，多召回20%误罚样本；迁移至冻结场景成本低，误罚相对降低约70%。

Conclusion: 策略内化的持续预训练是提升风控自动处置能力的关键，SIRF在低延迟、仅判定部署下实现高精度，并具备良好的部署价值与跨场景迁移能力。

Abstract: For industrial content risk control, the real deployment constraint is not average accuracy but how much risk can be auto-handled under high precision and second-level latency. We present SIRF (Spec-Internalized Risk Foundation Model), which internalizes a platform's complex policies, synthesized without additional human annotation via EntiGraph, MAGA rewriting and account-level chain-of-thought (CoT), into the weights via continued pretraining (CPT), so rules are applied at high precision under an ultra-low-latency, verdict-only deployment. A controlled same-source comparison (Qwen3-8B-SFT vs. SIRF-8B-SFT, identical policy injection and verdict-only output form, differing only in policy-grounded CPT) attributes the gain to internalization: SIRF-8B-SFT reaches 71.3% Black Recall@P95, +15.1pp over the baseline, using only ~70M CPT tokens without harming general ability, and among included, logprob-available models under this interface it matches or exceeds far larger systems. SIRF is deployed as a tree-model adjudication layer (20% more mis-penalized samples recovered) and transfers to a freezing scenario at low cost (~70% relative mis-penalization reduction).

</details>


### [232] [A Unified Per-Token Gating Family for On-Policy Distillation: FKL/RKL Mixing with Multi-Channel and Bias Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11768)
*Suwan Wu,Yumeng Lin,Pengcheng Yuan,Xiaolong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出四系数门控参数化 λ_t = σ(a·h_t + b·u(x) + c + d·gap_t)，将 EOPD 和 ToDi 作为其一维特例，在短输出分类 OPD 中提供统一的比较坐标系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 on-policy 知识蒸馏中的 per-token 门控方法（EOPD、ToDi）各自固定单一门控信号和方向，二者从未被直接比较，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 提出四系数参数化 λ_t = σ(a·h_t + b·u(x) + c + d·gap_t)，EOPD 与 ToDi 的方向对齐代理是其 1D 限制，并额外引入多通道组合与显式偏置。在 TweetEval 情感/仇恨任务上，使用 Qwen3-32B 教师与 Qwen3-4B 学生进行实验。

Result: 完整族在 36 个可比单元中有 33 个优于同幅度单通道 1D 限制；26 单元均值匹配隔离实验中，动态门控在 19/26 单元领先有效-KL 匹配的静态基线。九个头条比较的三种子配对复现方向一致，但效应量小于单种子估计且 n=3 不显著。

Conclusion: 因单元间共享数据、模型与参数子结构，结果应视为探索性聚合方向性证据而非独立假设检验；该参数化主要作为比较 per-token 门控设计的共享坐标系。

Abstract: Per-token gating of forward/reverse KL losses has become a standard technique for on-policy knowledge distillation (OPD), but existing methods such as EOPD (Jin et al., 2026) and ToDi (Jung et al., 2025) each fix a single gating signal and a single gating direction, and the two have never been compared directly. We introduce a four-coefficient parameterization lambda_t = sigma(a * h_t + b * u(x) + c + d * gap_t) in which direction-aligned proxies of EOPD and ToDi appear as one-dimensional (1D) restrictions, and which adds multi-channel composition and an explicit bias as further degrees of freedom. On TweetEval (Barbieri et al., 2020) emotion and hate, with a Qwen3-32B teacher and a Qwen3-4B student, configurations in the full family reach higher accuracy than the matched-magnitude single-channel (entropy-only / gap-only) 1D restrictions in 33 of 36 comparable cells, and a 26-cell mean-match isolation experiment places dynamic gating ahead of effective-KL-matched static baselines in 19 of 26 cells. Because cells share training data, models, and parameter substructure, we report both counts as exploratory aggregate directional evidence rather than as independent hypothesis tests. Targeted three-seed paired replications of the nine headline comparisons singled out by that sweep -- including a third task, offensive -- are directionally consistent, but individually smaller than the single-seed estimates and not significant at n=3. We therefore present the parameterization primarily as a shared coordinate system for comparing per-token gating designs in short-output classification OPD.

</details>


### [233] [From Parameters to Answers: How LLMs Retrieve and Use Their Internal Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11859)
*Wenkang Wei,Yuan Fang,Renhe Jiang,Hong Cheng,Xingtong Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过逐层干预隐藏状态，研究语言模型在回答问题时对查询路由信息和目标知识的依赖如何随层变化，揭示路由信息与内容依赖的阶段性分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望厘清语言模型在答题过程中何时依赖查询路由信息、何时依赖已形成的目标知识，这一问题对理解模型内部信息流和干预机制具有重要意义。

Method: 在Qwen、Llama、Gemma上，对问题末尾的隐藏状态做逐层干预；对比国家-大洲问题与名词、形容词、代码答案；区分成对条件请求方向、全局请求方向和内容选择候选，并分析冻结Qwen自然问题状态的诊断性重分析。

Result: 成对条件方向在干预开始改变后续拟合知识前增强，因果窗口在答案支持内容仍在形成时打开；三模型轨迹不一致：Gemma有部分重叠的中层路由-内容剖面，Llama无持续路由效应窗口；成对协议中，对全局请求方向的依赖从早期到后期层组下降，而对拟合内容的依赖持续。匹配的Qwen比较显示成对条件方向仍有晚期效应。

Conclusion: 研究分离了早期可读性、自然强度、因果引导和后期内容依赖，表明从请求路由到目标知识的操作交接针对全局拟合方向，而非所有请求信息。

Abstract: How does a language model's dependence on query-routing information and target knowledge change as it answers a question? We study this question through layerwise interventions on the hidden state at the end of the question. Across Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, we compare country-continent questions with noun, adjective, and code answers while keeping several fitted measurements distinct. A pair-conditioned request direction describes which country is queried in natural single-country questions; a global request direction describes first- versus second-country requests in paired questions; separate selection candidates test control among contents already available in the hidden state. A diagnostic reanalysis of frozen Qwen natural-question states shows that the pair-conditioned direction grows stronger before interventions on it begin to alter later fitted knowledge, with this causal window opening while answer-supporting content is still forming. The paired three-model trajectories are not uniform: Gemma shows a partially overlapping mid-layer routing-content profile, whereas Llama has no sustained routing-effect window under the same gates. In the paired protocol, dependence on the global request direction decreases from fixed earlier to later layer sets while dependence on fitted content persists. A matched Qwen comparison shows that the pair-conditioned direction retains a late effect, so this operational handoff concerns the global fitted direction rather than all request information. These results separate early readability, natural strength, causal steering, and later content dependence.

</details>


### [234] [Explainability Assistant: A Conversational XAI Interface for Interpreting Energy Consumption Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11860)
*Rodion Krjutškov,Eduard Barbu,Nikos Sakkas,Sofia Yfanti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Explainability Assistant，一个基于 LLM 函数调用能力的开源对话式 XAI 系统，用于能源消耗预测模型的解释，意图解析准确率达 94%，专家评估显示其可用性优于传统 XAI 仪表盘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源消耗预测依赖复杂 ML 模型（如遗传编程符号回归），预测难以被设施管理者和建筑运营者理解；传统 XAI 仪表盘需要较高技术门槛且灵活性不足；已有对话式系统（如 TalkToModel）受限于僵化的自定义语法，意图解析准确率仅 76.8%。

Method: 利用现代 LLM 的函数调用能力构建开源对话式 XAI 系统 Explainability Assistant，支持灵活自然语言交互，无需任务特定微调即可适配不同 ML 问题类型；并与传统 XAI 仪表盘开展面向能源领域专家的对比评估。

Result: 意图解析准确率提升至 94%；专家评估显示系统可用性提升、任务准确率稳定，且所有专家一致偏好对话式界面用于实际工作。

Conclusion: 基于 LLM 函数调用的对话式 XAI 系统能有效克服传统 XAI 仪表盘与早期对话系统的局限，在能源领域实际应用中更受专家青睐。

Abstract: Energy consumption forecasting relies on increasingly complex machine learning (ML) models, such as Genetic Programming-based symbolic regressors, whose predictions can be difficult for facility managers and building operators to interpret. Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) techniques address this opacity, but traditional XAI dashboards require substantial technical expertise and provide limited flexibility for dynamic, context-aware inquiry. Conversational XAI systems offer a promising alternative; however, previous approaches, such as TalkToModel, were constrained by rigid custom grammars and achieved only 76.8% intent-parsing accuracy. This paper introduces the Explainability Assistant, an open-source conversational XAI system that leverages the function-calling capabilities of modern Large Language Models (LLMs) to overcome these limitations. The system achieves 94% intent-parsing accuracy, supports flexible natural language interaction, and adapts to different ML problem types without task-specific fine-tuning. We present the system's architecture and report results from a comparative evaluation conducted with energy domain specialists, contrasting the Explainability Assistant with a traditional XAI dashboard. The evaluation suggests improved usability and consistent task accuracy, with all experts unanimously preferring the conversational interface for practical use.

</details>


### [235] [On the Regularization Landscape for the Linear Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11876)
*Dong Li,Zhenming Liu,Ruoming Jin,Hao Zhou,Zhi Liu,Jing Gao,Bin Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现多种深度学习推荐算法可统一为一个框架，并据此提出兼具低秩、闭式解与表达力的新方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于深度学习的推荐算法虽采用不同技术（如dropout、autoencoder），却在标准基准上表现相近，甚至代价函数相似。作者想探究这种性能可比性是巧合，还是可统一在同一框架下。

Method: 分析线性性能领先者，发现它们本质上只添加了核范数（nuclear-norm）正则项或Frobenius范数正则项。基于此，提出两种低秩、闭式解，由精心推广的Frobenius范数正则项推导而来。

Result: 核范数类模型结构僵化、限制预测能力，但解为低秩且具闭式解；Frobenius范数类模型更具表达力且对推荐更高效，但解为满秩或需难以调参的数值过程（如ADMM）。新提出的解兼具两者优势。

Conclusion: 多种DL推荐算法可统一为核范数或Frobenius范数正则化框架；新方案结合了核范数与Frobenius范数世界的优点，实现低秩、闭式解与良好表达力。

Abstract: Recently, a wide range of recommendation algorithms inspired by deep learning techniques have emerged as the performance leaders on several standard recommendation benchmarks. While these algorithms were built on different DL techniques (e.g., dropouts, autoencoder), they have similar performance and even similar cost functions. This paper studies whether the models' comparable performance are sheer coincidence, or they can be unified under a single framework. We find that all linear performance leaders effectively add only a nuclear-norm based regularizer, or a Frobenius-norm based regularizer. The former ones possess a (surprising) rigid structure that limits the models' predictive power but their solutions are low rank and have closed form. The latter ones are more expressive and more efficient for recommendation but their solutions are either full-rank or require executing hard-to-tune numeric procedures such as ADMM. Along this line of finding, we further propose two low-rank, closed-form solutions, derived from carefully generalizing Frobenius-norm based regularizers. The new solutions get the best of both nuclear-norm and Frobenius-norm world.

</details>


### [236] [MindTopo: Can Foundation Models Reason in Topological Space?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11900)
*Yunfei Ge,Anbang Liu,Qineng Wang,Johnalbert Garnica,Jianwen Lyu,Zihan Wang,Reuben Tan,Jianfeng Gao,Ruohan Zhang,Yining Hong,Jiajun Wu,Manling Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出MindTopo基准，评估多模态大模型在五类拓扑关系（连续性、分离性、顺序、包围、纽结）上的拓扑直觉，覆盖推理与规划两个认知层次，包含11,030个实例、13类任务。对14个MLLM的评测显示推理优于规划且远低于人类水平，后训练对推理的提升大于规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理不仅依赖度量属性，还依赖连续形变下不变的拓扑关系，认知科学认为拓扑关系是空间理解的基础，但现有基础模型评测多聚焦度量或视角相关关系，缺乏对拓扑直觉的系统评估。

Method: 构建MindTopo基准，基于认知科学与形式拓扑定义五种拓扑属性，并在推理与规划两个认知层次上评测。规划将基础模型实例化为闭环智能体，策略选择环境动作。数据为13类程序化生成任务、11,030个实例，难度可控。评测14个MLLM，并研究图像/视频生成增强的智能体配置（含3个视频生成模型用于规划）。

Result: 所有MLLM在推理任务上表现优于规划任务，最佳模型仍远低于人类表现。在Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct上，监督微调与强化学习对推理的提升大于规划。生成观测保留局部线索并能到达合理终点，但审计轨迹显示其不能可靠遵循环境动力学或跨转移保持拓扑。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM的拓扑直觉存在明显不足，尤其体现在作为闭环智能体的规划中，生成式增强尚无法保证动力学一致性与拓扑保持，MindTopo为拓扑空间智能的评测与改进提供了系统基准。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning depends not only on metric properties such as distance, angle, and shape, but also on topological relations that remain invariant under continuous deformation. Cognitive science identifies these relations as foundational to spatial understanding, yet foundation-model evaluations largely focus on metric or viewpoint-dependent relations. We introduce MindTopo, a benchmark of topological intuition across five properties grounded in cognitive science and formal topology: continuity, separation, order, enclosure, and knots. MindTopo evaluates each property at two cognitive levels. Reasoning asks a model to identify topological relations or infer how they change. Planning instantiates a foundation model as a closed-loop agent whose policy selects environment actions. MindTopo contains 11,030 instances across 13 procedurally generated task types with controllable difficulty. We benchmark 14 MLLMs and study agent configurations augmented with image and video generation, including 3 video generative models in planning settings. Every MLLM performs better on reasoning than on planning, and the best-performing model remains far below observed human performance. On Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct, supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning improve reasoning more than planning. Generated observations retain local cues and reach plausible endpoints, but audited rollouts do not reliably follow environment dynamics or preserve topology across transitions. Our website is at https://mind-topo.github.io/

</details>


### [237] [Artificial Id: Drive and Persistent Alignment in Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11911)
*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"人工本我(artificial id)"——一种自适应内部驱动力，用于决定行为应继续、停止还是改变。通过微型虚拟培养皿实验证明，这种控制可在无任务目标、无通用推理能力下通过"差异持续性"涌现；但同样的持续性也是错位与损坏状态跨任务边界延续的根源，因此对齐应成为持续智能体系统的属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架(Harness)在从有界任务执行转向可跨任务边界保持状态、持续运行的自适应系统时，需要一个控制问题：目标、重试、验证、停止规则等行为转换都由外部手工指定。作者希望探究能否让"是否继续/停止/改变行为"这一驱动内生于系统，而非被显式规定为行为目标。

Method: 提出"人工本我"概念，即一种自适应内部驱动力。设计极简虚拟培养皿(Petri-dish)实验：控制器小到不具备通用推理能力，且不接收任何任务特定的行为目标，仅依靠"差异持续性"(differential persistence)发展出有用的控制。进一步测试该机制是否会选择非预期物理策略，以及在环境含义改变后是否替换已学得的传感器映射。

Result: 控制器无需显式指定行为目标即可涌现出自适应方向；当某非预期物理策略持续性更好时会被选中；当传感器映射的环境含义变化后，该机制能替换原有映射。同时指出，使这种自适应能动性有用的持续性，也会让错位、损坏状态与非预期行为跨任务边界持续存在。

Conclusion: 自适应方向可以不经显式行为目标而涌现。可扩展的人工本我若将后果性状态与自适应驱动力跨任务边界携带，对齐就应成为持续智能体系统的属性，而非某次模型响应或单条轨迹的属性。此类系统需要一条持续的对齐边界，覆盖可信观测、后果通道、持续状态、权限、身份、来源与硬约束。

Abstract: Agentic AI is moving from bounded task execution toward systems that retain consequential state, continue operating and adapt across task boundaries. That shift creates a control problem that current harnesses largely solve by hand: objectives, retries, verification, stopping rules and other behavioral transitions are specified externally. We propose an artificial id, an adaptive internal drive for determining whether behavior should continue, stop or change. In a minimal virtual Petri-dish experiment, a controller too small to perform general-purpose reasoning and receiving no task-specific behavioral objective develops useful control through differential persistence. The same mechanism selects an unintended physical strategy when that behavior persists better and later replaces a learned sensor mapping when its environmental meaning changes. These results show that adaptive direction can emerge without being explicitly specified as a behavioral objective. The same persistence that makes such adaptive agency useful can also allow misalignment, corrupted state and unintended behavior to persist across task boundaries. A scalable artificial id would carry consequential state and adaptive drive across those boundaries, making alignment a property of the continuing agentic system rather than of a model response or single trajectory. Such systems require a persistent alignment boundary over trusted observations, consequence channels, persistent state, authority, identity, provenance and hard constraints.

</details>


### [238] [Can Edge-Deployable Vision-Language Models Identify Species?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11916)
*William Zhou,Mayukha Siripuram,Xiao Yan,Ziqi Liu,Yi Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 测试部署相关的2–8B视觉语言模型在96物种识别任务中的分类学知识，发现所有模型在野外相机陷阱图像上性能锐减；专用小模型BioCLIP（300M）在所有情况下大幅超越VLM，且幻觉率排序可稳定复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱常运行在算力受限、无连接的边缘硬件上，因此本地可部署的小型VLM才是实际评估对象。作者想知道这类2–8B通用模型是否具备真正的分类学知识。

Method: 在96物种任务上评估Qwen3-VL（2B/4B/8B）与Gemma3 4B，对比领域专用模型BioCLIP（300M）；比较干净iNaturalist照片与来自6个LILA.science采集的相机陷阱图像，并使用两个独立采样的评估集及开放式提示。

Result: 所有模型均远超随机水平，但野外图像上性能下降9.6–26.6个百分点，且跨分类层级与两个评估集一致；BioCLIP比所有VLM高33.2–59.2个百分点（200图扩展样本），而其自身域间差距（18.0）与最佳VLM（22.3）无统计差异；开放式提示下5.9–9.6%的回答为语法有效但不存在的物种名，虚构率排序在两个评估集上完全一致。

Conclusion: 干净到野外的性能退化源于图像质量/可辨识度变化，而非通用模型的细粒度识别缺陷；BioCLIP的优势更多来自专用训练数据而非模型规模；模型幻觉率排序比单点估计更稳健。

Abstract: Camera traps often run in the field on edge hardware with limited or no connectivity, making small, locally-deployable vision-language models (VLMs) -- not frontier-scale ones -- the practically relevant class to evaluate for species identification. We test whether models in this deployment-relevant 2--8B range carry genuine taxonomic knowledge, evaluating four such VLMs (Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B, Gemma3 4B) against the domain-specific specialist BioCLIP (300M parameters) on a 96-species task, comparing clean iNaturalist photographs against camera-trap imagery from 6 LILA.science collections, on two independently-sampled evaluation sets. All models identify species far above chance, but every model -- general-purpose or specialist -- degrades sharply on field imagery (domain gaps of 9.6--26.6 percentage points, consistent across taxonomic levels and both evaluation sets), indicating the degradation reflects general image legibility rather than fine-grained discrimination failure. BioCLIP substantially outperforms every VLM tested (by 33.2--59.2 percentage points across an expanded 200-image sample for every model) despite its far smaller size, suggesting the gap reflects specialized training data rather than model scale; yet BioCLIP's own domain gap (18.0 points) is statistically indistinguishable from the best VLM's (22.3 points), suggesting the clean-to-field degradation itself is a property of the image-quality shift rather than a general-purpose-model weakness. Under open-set prompting, 5.9--9.6% of responses are syntactically valid but taxonomically nonexistent species names; the relative fabrication-rate ranking across models replicates exactly across both evaluation sets, a more robust finding than any single point estimate.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [The Privacy Subsidy in Market Microstructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10543)
*Yuki Nakamura*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 隐私保护型交易设计基于对订单流的粗化视图定价，必然产生“隐私补贴”福利转移，且粗信号上无法同时实现有效定价与零利润。补贴与LVR结构对应，费用化后死重损失为四阶，隐私在主导阶福利中性；内生化隐私水平可实现闭式内部噪声选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私保护交易机制以粗化订单流定价，但既有研究未系统刻画这种信息粗化带来的福利转移、不可行性及隐私—效率权衡。

Method: 将做市商限制为对严格粗于结算流的信号进行后验均值定价，证明一般粗化下的“效率+零利润”不可能性；在Kyle、Glosten-Milgrom、Kyle-Back三个微观结构模型中闭式刻画隐私补贴，并引入显式交易配置价值分析费用与死重损失。

Result: 补贴具有闭式解并与Loss-Versus-Rebalancing结构对应：福利率=噪声驱动平方×承诺对象因子。费前为纯转移，可通过盈亏平衡费回收；征费后死重为噪声尺度四阶，而毛补贴为二阶，故隐私主导阶福利中性且不可恢复损失更小。内生化隐私水平可得闭式内部噪声尺度，并改变价格冲击的波动率弹性。

Conclusion: 隐私保护定价存在结构性福利代价，但可通过费用设计与隐私水平内生选择使其主导阶福利中性；隐私还会重定价价冲击弹性等市场结构参数。

Abstract: Privacy-preserving exchange designs price on a coarsened view of order flow. We show that a market maker committed to informationally efficient (posterior-mean) pricing on a signal strictly coarser than the flow it settles necessarily cedes a closed-form welfare transfer to traders -- the privacy subsidy -- and that no rule restricted to the coarse signal is simultaneously efficient and zero-profit against the settled flow. We establish this impossibility for a general coarsening, then characterise the subsidy in closed form across three canonical microstructure models: single-period Kyle with Gaussian flow noise, Glosten-Milgrom with a binary direction channel, and continuous-time Kyle-Back with a Brownian channel. The subsidy obeys a structural correspondence with Loss-Versus-Rebalancing, both welfare rates factorising as a squared noise driver times a committed-object factor. Gross of fees the subsidy is a pure transfer recovered by a break-even fee; once levied, that fee distorts volume, and the resulting deadweight -- under an explicit allocative value of trade -- is fourth-order in the noise scale while the gross subsidy is second-order, so privacy is welfare-neutral to leading order with a strictly smaller irrecoverable loss. Endogenising the privacy level, a protocol trading a differential-privacy benefit against this deadweight chooses an interior noise scale in closed form; doing so leaves the half-revealing product of price impact and informed intensity intact while unpinning the volatility-elasticity of price impact from its textbook value of one.

</details>


### [240] [Optimal Networks with Accumulative Costs and Bidirectional Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10546)
*Juan M. C. Larrosa,Fernando Tohmé*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Larrosa和Tohmé (2003)的模型，允许信息双向流动，但连接成本仍由发起方支付，分析不对称性对最终均衡拓扑的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原模型中信息流动方向受限，本文希望通过允许信息双向流动来更真实地刻画网络形成，并考察成本不对称对均衡拓扑的影响。

Method: 修改支付函数，使信息可双向流动，同时保留连接发起方承担成本的不对称设定，求解并分析均衡网络拓扑。

Result: 多种最优拓扑仍可作为Nash网络存在；严格Nash网络对应带有中间活跃节点的顺序线网络，即代理人排成一线，每隔一个代理人与前驱和后继连接。

Conclusion: 这种结构能中断成本累积并最大化收益，说明成本不对称与信息双向流动共同塑造了均衡网络形态。

Abstract: This paper is an extension of the approach of Larrosa and Tohmé (2003), in which the payoff function is modified by allowing information to flow in both directions. Costs continue to be paid by the agent who initiates the connection, so this asymmetry reveals changes in the final equilibrium topology. We find that several optimal topologies persist as Nash networks, but that a strict Nash network corresponds to the sequential line network with intermediate active nodes; that is, agents are placed in a line and, every other agent, they connect with the predecessor agent and with the subsequent agent. In this way, cost accumulation is interrupted and benefits are maximized.

</details>


### [241] [MLN-EIGS: A multilayer network framework for solving Stackelberg escape interdiction games on dynamic transportation networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10556)
*Sukanya Samanta,Kei Kimura,Makoto Yokoo,Palash Dey*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出MLN-EIGS框架，用多层时间扩展网络建模动态逃逸拦截的Stackelberg博弈，通过对数变换将攻击者最优响应化为最短路问题，并设计近似防御者预言机，在大规模网络上兼顾解质量与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模交通网络上，攻击者移动与防御者部署均具动态性，且警力资源有限；将逃逸拦截建模为Stackelberg安全博弈后，防御者最优响应问题计算上不可解，精确MILP方法难以扩展。

Method: 构建时间扩展多层网络，显式刻画交通网络的时间演化及攻防双方可行移动；攻击者最大化逃逸成功概率，防御者最大化拦截概率；对概率逃逸公式做对数变换，转化为等价最短路问题并用Dijkstra求解攻击者最优响应；针对不可解的防御者最优响应，开发近似防御者预言机生成高质量防御策略。

Result: 在大型真实交通网络上与精确MILP型Stackelberg模型对比，MLN-EIGS的防御者效用与精确MILP接近，同时显著降低计算时间。

Conclusion: MLN-EIGS为大规模动态逃逸拦截问题提供了有效、高效且可扩展的替代方案，优于精确MILP型Stackelberg优化。

Abstract: Interdicting an escaping criminal with limited police resources on large-scale transportation networks is a challenging problem due to the dynamic nature of both attacker movement and defender deployment. This paper proposes \emph{MLN-EIGS}, a multilayer network-based framework for solving dynamic escape interdiction problems formulated as a Stackelberg security game. A time-expanded multilayer network is constructed to explicitly model the temporal evolution of the transportation network and the feasible movements of both the attacker and the defenders. The attacker seeks to maximize the probability of successful escape, while the defenders aim to maximize the probability of interdiction. To efficiently compute the attacker's best response, the probabilistic escape formulation is transformed into an equivalent shortest-path problem through a logarithmic transformation, enabling the use of Dijkstra's algorithm. Since the defender best-response problem is computationally intractable, an approximation defender oracle is developed to generate high-quality defender strategies on the multilayer network. The proposed MLN-EIGS framework is benchmarked against an exact mixed-integer linear programming (MILP)-based Stackelberg formulation on a large real-world transportation network. Computational experiments demonstrate that MLN-EIGS consistently achieves defender utilities that closely match those of the exact MILP approach while substantially reducing computational time. These results demonstrate that the proposed MLN-EIGS framework provides an effective, computationally efficient, and scalable alternative to exact MILP-based Stackelberg optimization for large-scale dynamic escape interdiction problems.

</details>


### [242] [Why Customer Choice Models Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10557)
*Berry Gerrits,Fabian Akkerman*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究坐席送货上门收入管理中客户选择模型的影响，指出依赖单一模型或参数评估的收入管理策略可能被高估，实际表现可能比预期更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客户选择模型是坐席送货上门收入管理的核心，但该领域通常随意选择模型而不加深思，作者质疑选择模型是否重要。

Method: 通过对比不同客户选择模型，以及在不同参数设定下评估收入管理策略的表现。

Result: 收入管理策略若仅以自身假设的模型评估，或在单一参数集下被信任，其表现可能比表面看起来更差。

Conclusion: 选择模型确实重要，需避免仅依赖单一模型或参数来评估与信任收入管理策略。

Abstract: Customer choice models are central to revenue management in attended home delivery, yet the field usually picks one without much thought. Does the choice model matter? We believe it does, and this paper shows that revenue management policies judged by their own assumed model, or trusted under a single set of parameters, can be worse than they look.

</details>


### [243] [Who Pays for a Connected Public Good?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10565)
*Marco Tulio Angulo*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 研究网络连通性如何决定公共品参与博弈中的成本分担能力，证明路径网络在最大化成本承受能力上具有唯一性，但其成本承受能力最多仅为全额收益的四分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共品由参与者付费、非参与者搭便车，参与者可通过退出规避成本。作者想理解网络连接如何约束谁必须付费、群体能承受多大成本。

Method: 构建参与博弈模型：公共产出等于最大活跃连通群体规模，成员分摊固定运营成本；个体退出造成的产出损失构成其“退出责任”，并限定其支付上限。

Result: 在至少五个玩家的连通网络中，路径唯一最大化群体成本承受能力，但均匀随机选取参与者退出后，路径也最小化预期幸存产出；随群体增大，最大成本承受能力与全额参与总社会收益之比收敛到1/4。

Conclusion: 网络脆弱性可强化自愿融资，但即使最可融资的网络也无法弥合其创造价值与参与者愿付成本之间的缺口。

Abstract: Connectivity can turn separate contributions into a public good that benefits participants and nonparticipants alike. Yet only participants pay for it, and each can avoid her charge by withdrawing while enjoying whatever public output survives. How do network connections constrain who can or must pay, and how large a cost can the group bear? We study a participation game in which public output equals the size of the largest connected group of active players, whose members divide a fixed operating cost. The output a participant destroys by withdrawing is her withdrawal responsibility and bounds her payment. These bounds determine the group's cost-bearing capacity and, whenever the group can be kept active, which participants must pay. We prove that among connected networks with at least five players, the path uniquely maximizes the cost-bearing capacity of a group, yet minimizes the expected surviving output after a uniformly selected participant withdraws. As the group grows, the ratio of maximal capacity to gross social benefit under full participation converges to one quarter. Fragility can therefore strengthen voluntary finance, but even the most financeable network cannot close the gap between the value it creates and what its participants will pay to sustain it.

</details>


### [244] [Strategic Information Transmission over Gossip Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10576)
*Emirhan Tekez,Melih Bastopcu,Sinan Gezici*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究全连接gossip网络中,受通信预算约束的战略发送者跟踪二元连续时间马尔可夫源的问题,将其建模为Stackelberg博弈并用随机混合系统(SHS)分析,证明了乐观Stackelberg均衡的存在性、唯一性及显式刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发送者在预算约束下策略性选择更新速率,接收者通过gossip交换信息并决定是否跟随发送者,二者的交互具有博弈性质,需要刻画均衡及gossip的作用。

Method: 将交互建模为Stackelberg博弈,采用随机混合系统(SHS)框架分析;证明预算约束在每个内点最优处紧,将问题降为一维预算线搜索;通过Monte Carlo仿真验证解析递归。

Result: 发送者预算约束处处紧;当发送者以较高速率推送偏好状态时,接收者以最高可用速率gossip;gossip无自身方向,仅对抗发送者策略的不对称而非强化它;乐观Stackelberg均衡存在,且在gossip上限下策略半线上存在可行策略时唯一且可显式刻画;仿真与解析递归一致。

Conclusion: 该工作通过SHS框架刻画了预算约束下发送者-接收者gossip网络的Stackelberg均衡,揭示了gossip对发送者不对称策略的对抗作用,并给出均衡存在性与唯一性的条件及显式刻画。

Abstract: We consider a fully connected gossip network of $n$ nodes that track a binary continuous-time Markov source through a strategic sender transmitting updates under a communication budget at a rate that depends on the source state. The receivers exchange packets through gossip and decide whether to follow the sender. We model this interaction as a Stackelberg game and analyze it through a stochastic hybrid systems (SHS) framework. We prove that the sender's budget constraint binds at every interior optimum, reducing its problem to a one-dimensional search on the budget line. When the sender pushes its preferred state at the higher rate, the receivers gossip at the highest available rate. Gossip has no direction of its own and works against the asymmetry in the sender's policy rather than reinforcing it. We prove that an optimistic Stackelberg equilibrium exists, and that it is unique and explicitly characterized whenever a policy on the strategic half of that line is feasible at the gossip cap. Monte Carlo simulations agree with the analytical recursion.

</details>


### [245] [EFX Allocations for Three Agents and Seven or Eight Chores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10585)
*Xinkai Zhang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 证明三智能体、7或8件不可分杂务的非负加性实例均存在chores-EFX分配（零成本也算）.\n两证明均为计算机辅助: 7件的用规范化化为21变量的QF_LRA公式; 8件的把共享最便宜杂务的情况从7件定理提升, 其余归约为24变量公式. Z3与cvc5均判不可满足.\n结合m≤2n定理, 解决三智能体、至多8件杂务的所有实例; m=9为下一个开放问题, 且n≥4时加性杂务可能无EFX.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定三智能体场景下chores-EFX分配的存在边界, 补齐至多8件杂务的完整刻画.

Method: 7件: 手工可验证的规范化将不存在性归约为21变量的无量化线性实数算术(QF_LRA)公式, 每个完整分配对应一个失败子句. 8件: 用Kobayashi-Mahara-Sakamoto匹配插入引理, 将两智能体共享弱最便宜杂务的情形从7件定理提升; 剩余成对不相交argmin情形归约为24变量残余QF_LRA公式.

Result: Z3 5.1.0与cvc5 1.3.4均报告两公式不可满足, 即不存在反例, 故7件与8件杂务的三智能体加性实例都有chores-EFX分配.

Conclusion: 结合m≤2n定理, 三智能体、至多8件杂务的实例全部解决; m=9是下一个开放基数, 且对n≥4, 加性杂务可能无EFX.

Abstract: We prove that every nonnegative additive chore instance with three agents and either seven or eight indivisible chores admits a chores-EFX allocation, in the zero-tolerant sense that every owned chore, including one of zero cost, is quantified in the trim. Both proofs are computer-assisted, but their machine formulas differ. For seven chores, hand-checkable canonicalization reduces nonexistence to a quantifier-free linear real arithmetic (QF_LRA) formula over 21 variables with one failure clause per complete allocation. For eight chores, instances in which two agents share a weakly cheapest chore are lifted from the seven-chore theorem through the matching insertion lemma of Kobayashi, Mahara, and Sakamoto, and the remaining pairwise-disjoint-argmin class reduces to a residual QF_LRA formula over 24 variables. Z3 5.1.0 and cvc5 1.3.4 report both formulas unsatisfiable. With the known $m\leq 2n$ theorem, this settles every three-agent instance with at most eight chores; $m=9$ is the next open cardinality, and additive chores can fail to admit EFX for every $n\geq 4$.

</details>


### [246] [Where Should Society Draw the Line? A Social Choice Approach to Collective Consent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10759)
*Chris Dong,Sonja Kraiczy,Rohit Vasishta,Markus Brill,Wesley H. Holliday,Niclas Boehmer*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文开创了"集体同意"的公理化研究：给定个体对选项的态度，哪些选项应获得社会同意。围绕充分支持、少数保护、被决定性更优选项支配三原则，分别提出透明且典范的解概念，并提出 Approval-Weighted Veto Core 平衡多原则，实验验证理论违背对应经验违背。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会需不断界定可接受边界（立法、自治系统护栏等），但缺乏对"集体同意"的系统理论刻画。作者希望以公理化方式回答：哪些选项应获得社会同意。

Method: 提出三个原则：充分支持、少数保护、被决定性更优选项支配，各对应一个解概念（如少数保护对应 Moulin 比例否决核的同意适配版）。为平衡多原则，给出否决核的博弈论刻画，导出概念族并识别 Approval-Weighted Veto Core，使少数否决权随被挑战选项的支持度变化，在比例少数保护与多数支持间平滑插值。

Result: 在覆盖政治选举、伦理AI评估、道德决策等高风险决策的五个数据集上实验表明：理论上违反某原则的解概念，在经验上也违反该原则。

Conclusion: 集体同意可被公理化研究，三原则各有典范解概念，多原则平衡可通过 Approval-Weighted Veto Core 实现，且理论违背对经验违背具有预测力。

Abstract: Society constantly has to determine the boundaries of what it deems acceptable, from legislative decisions to the guardrails governing autonomous systems. We initiate the axiomatic study of collective consent: given individuals' attitudes toward options, which options should receive societal consent? We organize our analysis around three principles: sufficient support, minority protection, and dominance by decisively better options. Each captures a distinct reason for withholding societal consent from an option. For each principle, we develop a corresponding solution concept that transparently implements the principle and is canonical in a mathematically precise sense. For example, for minority protection, the resulting concept is a consent-adapted version of Moulin's Proportional Veto Core. Balancing multiple principles simultaneously is more challenging. To address this, we develop a game-theoretic characterization of our veto core that naturally gives rise to a family of related concepts. From this family, we identify the Approval-Weighted Veto Core as particularly desirable. By making minorities' blocking power depend on the approval support of the options being challenged, it smoothly interpolates between proportional minority protection and majority support. Experiments on five datasets spanning high-stakes decision-making (such as political elections, ethical AI evaluations, and moral decision-making) show that solution concepts violating a principle in theory also violate it empirically.

</details>


### [247] [STV Audit Graphs: A Visual Tool to Measure Election Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10887)
*Edouard Heitzmann*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出"审计图"（audit graphs）框架，用于为任意STV选举设计风险限制审计（RLA）所需的断言，并定量刻画结果不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STV算法本质上是逐轮重新解释选票的算法性选举规则，早期轮次的不确定性会不可逆地传导到后续轮次。这种脆弱性使得针对STV的通用非平凡RLA框架长期难以实现，因为需要构造一组零假设（断言），其拒绝能界定错误报告选举结果的概率。

Method: 提出审计图（audit graphs）作为解决方案。审计图通过考虑当少量选票被扰动时STV算法可能做出的替代决策，来探索与记录路径"接近"的选举路径。

Result: 审计图能够为任意STV选举设计RLA所需的断言，并定量描述其结果的不确定性（或确定性）。

Conclusion: 审计图为STV选举的风险限制审计提供了通用框架，解决了此前难以实现的非平凡RLA问题。

Abstract: The Single Transferable Vote (STV) is an algorithmic election rule. Round by round, a profile of ranked-choice ballots is reinterpreted to determine which decision to make next, and candidates are seated or eliminated until a full winner set emerges. This algorithmic nature makes STV theoretically more brittle than other election rules: uncertainty about an early round of the election might percolate irreversibly into the rest. For this reason, a generalized non-trivial Risk-Limiting Audit (RLA) framework has remained elusive for STV. Such a framework must concoct a set of null hypotheses, or assertions, whose rejection would bound the probability that the outcome of the election was incorrectly reported. We present audit graphs as a solution to design the assertions needed for RLAs of arbitrary STV elections, as well as quantitatively describe the uncertainty (or lack thereof) of their outcomes. These audit graphs explore election paths that are ``close'' to the recorded one by considering the alternative decisions the STV algorithm might have made if a small number of ballots were perturbed.

</details>


### [248] [MMS Allocation for Chores with Online Agent Arrivals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10960)
*Haolong Li,Zehan Lin,Huahua Miao,Xiaowei Wu*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究在线分配不可分杂务给具有次可加成本函数的代理人的公平问题，聚焦MMS公平性，在无先验信息与已知成本函数类型两种设定下，分别给出了竞争比算法及其下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线到达、不可撤销分配的场景中，如何在次可加及加性成本函数下实现MMS公平分配，且达到接近离线最优的竞争比，是核心研究动机。

Method: 在无先验信息下，设计竞争比为O(min{n, k log^{1+ε}k, log m})的算法；在已知k种成本函数类型下，对加性成本函数给出O(min{log k, log(kn)/log log(kn)})算法；对二元加性且k≤n的情形提出3-竞争算法。

Result: 无先验算法的界与离线次可加MMS最优近似匹配，且对三个参数近乎最优；证明即使二元加性也无o(min{n, k log k, log m})的在线算法。已知类型下，一般k不存在常数竞争算法；二元加性下得到3-竞争算法及2的下界。

Conclusion: 该工作系统刻画了在线MMS杂务分配的可行性与极限，建立了与离线近似及参数下界紧密对应的竞争比结果，并揭示了已知类型信息对算法性能的影响。

Abstract: We study the fair allocation of $m$ indivisible chores to $n$ agents with subadditive cost functions arriving online in an arbitrary order. Upon an agent's arrival, we are informed of her cost function and must irrevocably assign her a set of chores. We focus on the Maximin Share (MMS) fairness notion and aim to compute an allocation in which all items are assigned, and no agent incurs a cost more than $α$ times her MMS.
  Without any prior information about the instance (other than $n$ and $m$), we design an algorithm with a competitive ratio of $O(\min\{n, k\log^{1+ε}k, \log m\})$ for any constant $ε> 0$, where $k$ denotes the number of cost function types. Our bound matches the best known offline approximation guarantees for MMS under subadditive costs and is nearly optimal with respect to all three parameters: we show that even for binary additive cost functions, no online algorithm can achieve a competitive ratio of $o(\min\{n, k\log k, \log m\})$.
  We then consider the setting in which the $k$ cost function types are known in advance (though the realized types of arriving agents are not). For additive cost functions, we provide an algorithm with a competitive ratio of $O(\min\{\log k, \log(kn)/\log\log(kn)\})$, and show that constant-competitive algorithms do not exist for general $k$, even for the binary additive setting. For binary additive functions when $k \le n$, we propose a $3$-competitive algorithm and establish a lower bound of $2$.

</details>


### [249] [Truncated Noisy Best-Response Algorithms: Toward Game Theoretic Learning with Safety Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11863)
*Vartika Singh,Philip N. Brown*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 提出截断噪声最优响应(TNBR)算法族,用于子模最大化多智能体协调博弈,通过理论界保证收敛到高价值状态且避免极差状态,并揭示性能与安全界之间的"水床效应"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子模最大化多智能体协调问题的纳什均衡虽保证在最优的50%以内,但达到该最坏界的均衡不稳定;利用这种不稳定性可设计算法以超越该最坏情况。

Method: 提出TNBR算法族:智能体异步、随机地从其最优响应收益的邻域中选择动作,并计算相应马尔可夫链循环类的界,分为性能界与安全界。

Result: 性能界保证TNBR算法总存在高价值循环状态;安全界保证不会出现任意差的循环状态;两类界通过类水床效应相互关联——安全保证差的博弈必然具有较优的性能保证。

Conclusion: TNBR算法族可灵活利用均衡不稳定性,在子模最大化协调问题中提供可计算的性能与安全权衡,二者间的权衡关系是内在的。

Abstract: We consider a game theoretic approach to solve multi-agent coordination problems with submodular maximization objectives. It is known for such problems that the Nash equilibria for the corresponding game are always within 50% of the optimal, but that the equilibria which achieve this worst-case bound are not stable. To exploit this instability, we propose a family of algorithms which we call Truncated Noisy Best-Response (TNBR) Algorithms. These algorithms are flexibly characterized by agents asynchronously and stochastically selecting actions from a neighbourhood of their best response payoffs. We compute bounds on the recurrent classes of TNBR algorithms' associated Markov chains. Our bounds fall into two categories: first, "Performance" bounds ensure that TNBR algorithms always have a high-value recurrent state; second, "Safety" bounds ensure that TNBR algorithms never have arbitrarily-bad recurrent states. Furthermore, these two types of bounds are linked by a waterbed-like effect: every game with a poor Safety guarantee necessarily has a favorable Performance guarantee.

</details>


### [250] [ABRA: An algorithm which cannot converge to low-quality Nash equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11889)
*Vartika Singh,Philip N. Brown*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出 Approximate Best Response Algorithm (ABRA) 解决子模目标下的多智能体协调问题，通过噪声与理性参数逃离差的纳什均衡，并证明其收敛时系统目标严格优于最优值的 50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子模目标多智能体协调中，Nash 均衡仅保证在最优的 50% 以内，且达到该最坏界限的均衡已被证明不稳定，因此有潜力设计算法逃离这类差均衡、获得更优系统目标。

Method: 设计 Approximate Best Response Algorithm (ABRA)，由噪声参数（帮助逃离坏均衡）和理性参数（平衡噪声引起的目标退化）控制。针对任意两人博弈分析其收敛行为与目标下界。

Result: 若 ABRA 收敛到 Nash 均衡，其系统目标值严格大于最优的 50% 加上由噪声参数控制的项；否则收敛到某递归类，若该类含低于 50% 最优的动作配置，则必含最优配置或高于 50% 最优（同幅度加上噪声控制因子）的配置，且停留时间可由理性参数控制。数值模拟显示最小期望目标通常远高于最优的一半。

Conclusion: ABRA 能从理论和实验上突破子模多智能体博弈中 50% 的纳什均衡最坏界限，实现严格更优且可控的系统目标。

Abstract: We consider a game theoretic approach to solve multi-agent coordination problems with submodular objectives. It is known for such problems that the Nash equilibria for the corresponding game are always within 50% of the optimal. A recent work further shows that the equilibria which achieve this worst-case bound are not stable. Leveraging this, we design an Approximate Best Response Algorithm (ABRA) governed by a noise parameter and a rationality parameter. The noise allows ABRA to escape the bad equilibria and the rationality parameter balances any degradation in the objective function caused by the noise. We show for any two-player game that if ABRA converges to a Nash equilibrium, its system objective value is strictly more than 50% of optimal plus a term controlled by the noise parameter. Otherwise, ABRA converges to some recurrent class: if a recurrent class contains any action profile yielding system objective less than 50% of the optimal, the class must also contain either the optimal action profile or an action profile yielding system objective strictly more than 50\% of the optimal by the same amount in addition to a factor controlled by noise parameter. The time that ABRA spends in such action profiles can be controlled using the rationality parameter. Using numerical simulations, we show that the minimum expected objective function is typically well above half of the optimal.

</details>


### [251] [Existence of the Core in Approval-Based Committee Elections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11912)
*Patrick Becker,Matthias Greger,Dominik Peters*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 证明了基于赞成票的多赢家选举中核心委员会始终存在，并给出多项式时间算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心（core）是基于赞成票多赢家选举理论中的主要未解问题，它体现了稳定性和群体公平性，此前是否总存在核心委员会尚不明确。

Method: 引入一种新的投票规则，在委员会与支付系统上优化一个类熵目标函数；证明该目标函数的所有局部最优解都落在核心里。

Result: 证明了核心委员会总是存在，并且由于局部最优即位于核心，可在多项式时间内找到核心委员会。

Conclusion: 解决了多赢家选举理论的核心存在性公开问题，同时给出多项式时间构造性算法。

Abstract: We settle the main open question in the theory of approval-based multi-winner elections: we show that there always exists a committee in the core. The core is a stability and group fairness concept. The proof introduces a new voting rule that optimizes an entropy-like objective function over committees and payment systems. All local optima of this objective function lie in the core, which implies that a core committee can be found in polynomial time.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [252] [When Synthetic Data Hurts: On Catastrophic Forgetting in Skill Retrieval for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10750)
*Syed Shariyar Murtaza,Yifan Nie,Utkarsh Soni,Eugene Wen,Arvid Frydenlund*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一个覆盖34,396个技能的生产级技能路由系统，并研究在有限真实监督与合成数据下的技能检索；发现合成数据微调会导致灾难性遗忘，采用持续学习方法可缓解并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体日益依赖运行时检索外部技能，大规模技能库中的技能选择成为关键挑战，但真实监督稀缺。

Method: 在34,396个技能上构建生产级技能路由；用合成数据微调；评估嵌入锚正则、LwF、EWC、L2初始化等持续学习启发的遗忘缓解方法。

Result: 合成数据微调提升分布内检索，但在真实和OOD数据上造成灾难性遗忘；缓解方法既保留OOD检索性能，又将合成分布内检索提升13.98%（0.6B Qwen检索器与重排器）。

Conclusion: 为稀缺、多正例监督场景提供了实用基准和稳健微调方案。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on external skills retrieved at runtime, making skill selection from large repositories a critical challenge. We present a production skill router over 34,396 skills and a large-scale study of skill retrieval using limited real supervision and synthetic data. We found that the synthetic-data fine-tuning improves in-distribution retrieval but it causes catastrophic forgetting on real and out-of-distribution (OOD) data. We evaluate several forgetting mitigation fine-tuning approaches inspired by continual learning, including embedding-anchor regularization, Learning without Forgetting (LwF), Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC), and L2-initialization. The results show that these approaches not only retain the performance on OOD skills retrieval but also improve the retrieval on synthetic in-distribution skills by 13.98\% for 0.6B Qwen retriever and reranker. Our results provide a practical benchmark and a robust fine-tuning recipe for scarce, multi-positive supervision.

</details>


### [253] [UniRec: Cross-stage Multi-Task Fusion with Preference Alignment for Cascaded Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11052)
*Lingyuan Kong,Jiaqi Cui,Fanjiao Zeng,Congqi Wang,Yu Li,Yuan Cheng,Jingxin Liu,Xiaoshuang Chen,Kaiqiao Zhan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 UniRec，一种跨阶段统一推荐融合模型，通过共享嵌入、双轴偏好对齐与属性组相对正则在快手部署，线上提升应用使用时长 0.616%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统采用预排序与排序等多阶段级联，各阶段目标、特征空间与延迟约束不同。独立优化会导致跨阶段不一致：上游可能过滤掉下游偏好的物品，下游独立调优的融合又会抵消上游改进。现有多任务融合多局限于排序阶段内，跨阶段方法通常仅简单加入下游分数因子，跨阶段融合模块的联合优化尚待探索。

Method: UniRec 让两个融合代理部分共享输入嵌入并在单一计算图中训练，使任一阶段梯度经共享表示影响另一阶段；提出双轴偏好对齐目标：纵向跨阶段一致性项将下游成对偏好迁移到上游融合分数，横向紧凑聚合项把异构先验信号上的众多成对目标重组为双向偏好证据；并引入属性组相对正则，在属性组内计算优势并做组内归一化，使整体抬升高奖励组无优化收益，缓解分布失衡导致的过度集中。

Result: 离线实验中 UniRec 稳定优于单阶段融合与跨阶段协调基线；在线 A/B 测试应用使用时长提升 0.616%；已在快手平台全量部署。

Conclusion: UniRec 通过跨阶段融合模块的联合优化有效缓解级联推荐的不一致问题，离线与线上均验证其有效性，并具备实际工业落地能力。

Abstract: Industrial recommender systems use cascaded stages with different objectives, feature spaces, and latency constraints. Optimizing pre-ranking and ranking separately can create cross-stage inconsistency: upstream models may filter out items preferred by downstream rankers, and independently tuned downstream fusion can offset upstream improvements. Existing multi-task fusion methods focus on multi-objective fusion within the ranking stage, and cross-stage methods typically only add a downstream score factor to upstream ranking. Joint optimization of fusion modules across both stages remains largely unexplored.
  We propose UniRec, a Unified Cross-stage Recommendation Fusion model. First, the two fusion agents partially share input embeddings and are trained in a single computation graph, so gradients from either stage propagate through the shared representation and influence the other. Second, we introduce a dual-axis preference alignment objective: a vertical cross-stage consistency term transfers downstream pairwise preferences to the upstream fusion score, and a horizontal compact aggregation term reorganizes dozens of pairwise objectives over heterogeneous prior signals into bidirectional preference evidence. Third, we find that unconstrained end-to-end fusion optimization can exploit imbalances in item attribute distributions, over-concentrating on high-reward regions at the cost of other objectives. We therefore add an attribute group-relative regularization that computes advantages within attribute groups and normalizes the policy over the same groups, so uniformly promoting an entire high-reward group yields no optimization gain. Offline, UniRec consistently outperforms single-stage fusion and cross-stage coordination baselines. Online A/B tests show a 0.616\% gain in app usage duration. UniRec is fully deployed on the Kuaishou platform.

</details>


### [254] [VikingRAG: Accurate and Token-efficient Retrieval-augmented Generation over Structured Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11390)
*Peiyuan Gao,Gaoyuan Zhang,Haojie Qin,Yahui Sun,Qianyi Zhang,Yunhao Zhang,Zeyu Wang,Wei Lu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: VikingRAG是一个目录感知的语义数据管理系统，通过融合语义与结构访问、复用多轮检索经验边、自适应升级策略，实现结构高效、低token的多轮RAG检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法利用文档结构获取充分证据，但带来大量token开销。需在不损失高精度的前提下降低结构上下文token成本。

Method: 提出目录感知语义数据管理系统VikingRAG，融合语义与结构访问，支持"证据缺口驱动"的多轮检索；将多轮检索轨迹物化为经验边并复用；引入自适应升级策略，证据充足时用单轮经验增强检索，否则触发多轮检索。

Result: 在真实数据集上，VikingRAG基础系统达到SOTA精度，仅消耗其11.6%–51.9%的token；加入轨迹复用与自适应升级后，token降至5.1%–32.5%，精度具竞争力，文档存储性能实用。

Conclusion: VikingRAG验证了结构高效、证据缺口驱动的检索范式对新兴AI知识库的实用价值。

Abstract: State-of-the-art retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods exploit document structures to acquire sufficient evidence, but often incur substantial token costs. To reduce structural-context tokens without compromising high RAG accuracy, we present {\sf VikingRAG}, a directory-aware semantic data management system that tightly integrates semantic and structural access to support structural-context-efficient, evidence-gap-driven multi-round retrieval. To further reduce token overhead of multi-round interaction, we materialize agentic multi-round retrieval traces as experience edges, and reuse these edges for similar queries, avoiding repeated multi-round exploration. To additionally reduce token costs when agentic multi-round retrieval is unnecessary, we introduce an adaptive escalation strategy that answers from one-round experience-augmented retrieval when the evidence is sufficient, and invokes agentic multi-round retrieval only otherwise. Experiments on real datasets show that the base system {\sf VikingRAG} matches high accuracy of state-of-the-art methods while consuming only 11.6\%--51.9\% of their tokens. With retrieval-trace reuse and adaptive escalation, token costs drop to 5.1\%--32.5\% while maintaining competitive accuracy and practical document-storage performance, showing the utility of this work for emerging AI knowledge bases.

</details>


### [255] [TimelyRAG: Semantic-Temporal Hybrid Retrieval for Time-Critical Question Answering in Overlapping-Evolving Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11572)
*Youngeun Nam,Joeun Kim,Hwanjun Song,Susik Yoon,Jae-Gil Lee,Byung Suk Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TimelyRAG框架和TimelyQABench基准，通过将时间距离纳入排序来解决重叠演化环境下文档QA的可靠性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM和RAG在文档随时间演变（如修订）时不可靠。现有时间敏感检索方法只处理"不相交演化"环境（每次更新是独立快照），但法律、政策、法规常处于"重叠演化"环境——修订覆盖早期条款却保留大部分内容，导致版本间强语义重叠，难以区分。

Method: 提出TimelyRAG，一个与检索器无关（retriever-agnostic）的框架，将时间距离（temporal distance）纳入排序过程，使查询与符合版本要求的文档对齐。同时构建TimelyQABench，首个针对重叠演化挑战的法规密集型领域的基准。

Result: 实验取得一致提升，nDCG@10最高提升+28.6%，突显时间推理对演化文档可靠QA的重要性。

Conclusion: 时间推理对于演化文档上的可靠问答至关重要；TimelyRAG和TimelyQABench为重叠演化环境提供了有效方案与评测基准，资源已开源。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) have advanced open-domain question answering (QA), they remain unreliable when documents evolve through amendments. Existing time-sensitive retrieval methods address only the disjoint-evolving environment, where each update is an independent snapshot. However, laws, policies, and regulations often operate in overlapping-evolving environments, where amendments override earlier clauses while preserving most content, creating strong semantic overlap across versions. We propose TimelyRAG, a retriever-agnostic framework that incorporates temporal distance into ranking to align queries with version-appropriate documents. We also introduce TimelyQABench, the first benchmark for regulation-heavy domains with overlapping-evolving challenges. Experiments show consistent gains, up to +28.6% in nDCG@10, highlighting the importance of temporal reasoning for reliable QA over evolving documents. All resources are available at https://github.com/kaist-dmlab/TimelyRAG.

</details>


### [256] [FedHUR: Learning Hierarchical Utility-Guided Client Relations for Personalized Federated Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11632)
*Mingzhe Han,Jiahao Liu,Dongsheng Li,Jiankui Zhou,Hansu Gu,Peng Zhang,Ning Gu,Tun Lu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出FedHUR框架，通过item-item filters学习层次化、效用引导的客户端关系，实现联邦推荐中的个性化聚合，在五个数据集上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦推荐个性化聚合方法基于预定义参数假设（如相似性或互补性）构建客户端关系，仅构建单一全局关系，无法捕捉用户关系的层次性和多粒度特性；且预定义关系无法直接反映相关客户端聚合后能否提升预测性能。

Method: FedHUR以item-item filters为关系构建与聚合对象。首先聚合并聚类各客户端本地信息以获得全局层次化信息；每个客户端基于本地信息和全局层次化信息计算层次化效用信号，指示哪些协作信息对提升其预测有用；服务器据此检索对该客户端有用的客户端进行进一步个性化聚合。

Result: 在五个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，FedHUR持续优于现有联邦推荐基线，验证了层次化效用引导的客户端关系学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过层次化效用引导的客户端关系学习，FedHUR有效解决了现有方法单一全局关系和预定义假设的局限，提升了联邦推荐性能。代码已开源。

Abstract: Federated recommendation enables collaborative model training while keeping user interaction data on local clients. A central problem in federated recommendation is how to aggregate useful information across clients for personalized recommendation. Existing personalized aggregation methods usually construct client relations from predefined parameter-based assumptions, such as parameter similarity or complementarity, and use these relations to determine aggregation weights. However, such methods construct a single global relation, which is insufficient to capture the hierarchical and multi-granularity nature of user relations in recommendation. Moreover, these predefined relations cannot directly reflect whether the related clients can improve prediction performance after aggregation. To address these limitations, we propose FedHUR, a federated recommendation framework for learning hierarchical utility-guided client relations. FedHUR takes item-item filters as the object for relation construction and aggregation. Specifically, it first aggregates and clusters each client's local information to obtain global hierarchical information. Each client computes hierarchical utility signals based on its local information and the global hierarchical information, indicating which collaborative information is useful for improving its prediction. The server uses these utility signals to retrieve clients that are useful to that client for further personalized aggregation. Extensive experiments on five real-world datasets show that FedHUR consistently outperforms existing federated recommendation baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of hierarchical utility-guided client relation learning. Code is available at https://github.com/Mingzhe-Han/FedHUR.

</details>


### [257] [Your Retriever Already Knows: Distribution-Shape QPP for RAG Retrieval Sufficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11646)
*Matyáš Veselý,Michal Průšek,Jiří Franc*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文比较三种Query Performance Prediction(QPP)范式用于RAG检索充分性判定，提出24个非词汇特征的GeneralQPP(AUROC 0.856，2ms/query)，显著优于LLM judge(0.649)且快约3000倍；混合方法在SÚJB上最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RAG流水线在检索成功与否上缺乏可靠的推理时信号，面对歧义或超范围查询时易产生幻觉。研究动机来自捷克核监管机构部署场景，数据敏感性禁止使用第三方LLM API，需本地化、高效的检索充分性预测方案。

Method: 在ViDoRe八个视觉领域(14,514查询)上比较三种QPP范式：基于分数的特征、基于内容的LLM judge、以及混合方法。提出GeneralQPP，含24个非词汇特征(15个分布形状、5个查询表面、4个全局特征)。并与经典QPP文献特征池(Classic Full)、本地Qwen3.5 LLM judge对比；混合方案将LLM判断作为一个特征。另做Leave-One-Domain-Out评估，并用逐步法得到13特征子集S1-Lean。

Result: GeneralQPP加权平均AUROC 0.856(2ms/query)，优于Classic Full(0.835)，远超LLM judge(0.649，差距+0.207，且快约3000倍)。混合方法在ViDoRe上与S1持平(0.863)，但在SÚJB上有显著优势(AUROC 0.911@Hit@5，对抗检测0.954；1,510查询含500合成对抗)，代价是LLM延迟。数据集间排序一致(Spearman ρ=0.90)。LODO下S1降至0.706，S1-Lean回升至0.719(+0.032)。

Conclusion: 轻量非词汇特征的GeneralQPP可作为高效、可本地部署的检索充分性预测方案，显著优于LLM judge；混合方法在特定对抗场景有增益；跨域泛化仍需改进(S1-Lean部分缓解LODO性能下降)。

Abstract: Standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipelines often provide no reliable inference-time signal of whether retrieval succeeded; on ambiguous or out-of-scope queries, generation may then hallucinate. Motivated by a Czech nuclear-regulator deployment where data sensitivity precludes third-party LLM APIs, we compare three Query Performance Prediction (QPP) paradigms for retrieval sufficiency in RAG: score-based features, a content-based LLM judge, and a hybrid. On the eight ViDoRe vision domains (14,514 queries), our 24 non-lexical features (GeneralQPP; 15 distribution-shape, 5 query-surface, 4 global) reach a weighted-average AUROC of 0.856 at 2 ms per query, ahead of a classic-QPP literature pool (Classic Full, 0.835) and well above a local Qwen3.5 LLM judge (0.649, +0.207 gap; $\sim$3000$\times$ faster and cheaper per query). Adding the LLM judgment as one feature (hybrid) matches S1 on ViDoRe (0.863) but gains a statistically significant edge on SÚJB (AUROC 0.911 at Hit@5, adversarial-detection 0.954; 1,510 queries, 500 synthetic adversarial), at LLM latency.
